VP 6.7.1

राज्यलाभाद्विना नान्यत्क्षत्रियाणा मतिप्रियम्

of 194 Sri Vishnu Puraanam – Amsha 6 केमशध्वज

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

rājylābhādvinā — of 194 Sri; nānytkṣtriyāṇā — Vishnu Puraanam –; mtipriym — Amsha 6 केमशध्वज

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

rājylābhādvinā nānytkṣtriyāṇā mtipriym ||1||

Śrīdhara commentary.

No commentary.

राज्यलाभाद्विना नान्यत्क्षत्रियाणा मतिप्रियम्

VP 6.7.2

राज्यमेतदशेषं ते यत्र गृध्नन्त्यपण्डिताः

॥ Sri Engal Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

rājymetdśeṣṃ — ॥; te — Sri; ytr — Engal; gṛdhnntypṇḍitāḥ — Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Śrīdhara

atrottrṃkeśidhvjety-ādi — Khaandikya Janaka -; ytḥ — O Keshidhvaja, do; kārṇāt — know why I did; tvdīyṃ — not ask you; rājyṃ — for a kingdom, where; myā — all the people; n — who do not have; prārthitm — the viveka go; td — and fight over kingdom; idṃ — and other things.; nibodh — I am not like; śṛṇu — that. Pandita means; ytr — - panda means; rājye — knowledge of what is; apṇḍitāḥ — right and what; avivekinḥ — is wrong. One who; bhog-lipsv — has this viveka,; ev — the mind which can; gṛdhnnti — distinguish between what; apekṣāṃ — is right and what; kurvnti — is wrong, is; 2 — a pandita. ## 3

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

rājymetdśeṣṃ te ytr gṛdhnntypṇḍitāḥ ||2||

Śrīdhara

atrottrṃ—keśidhvjety-ādi | ytḥ kārṇāt tvdīyṃ rājyṃ myā n prārthitm | td idṃ nibodh, śṛṇu | ytr rājye apṇḍitāḥ avivekinḥ bhog-lipsv ev gṛdhnnti apekṣāṃ kurvnti ॥2॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

अत्रोत्तरं—केशिध्वजेत्य्-आदि । यतः कारणात् त्वदीयं राज्यं मया न प्रार्थितम्। तद् इदं निबोध, शृणु । यत्र राज्ये अपण्डिताः अविवेकिनः भोग-लिप्सव एव गृध्नन्ति अपेक्षां कुर्वन्ति ॥२॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 2).

Khaandikya Janaka - O Keshidhvaja, do know why I did not ask you for a kingdom, where all the people who do not have the viveka go and fight over kingdom and other things. I am not like that. Pandita means - panda means knowledge of what is right and what is wrong. One who has this viveka, the mind which can distinguish between what is right and what is wrong, is a pandita. ## 3

VP 6.7.3

वधश्च धर्मयुद्धेन स्वराज्यपरिपन्थिनाम्

of 194 Sri Vishnu Puraanam – Amsha 6 ित्रत्रयाणामयं

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

vdhśc — of 194 Sri; dhrmyuddhen — Vishnu Puraanam –; svrājypripnthinām — Amsha 6 ित्रत्रयाणामयं

Śrīdhara

nnu — What; sv-dhrm-siddhye — you said; pṇḍitānām — is right,; api — the duty; rājy-kāṅkṣā — of a; yuktevety — Kshatriya is; uktṃ — looking after; ttrāhkṣtriyāṇām — the prajaas.; iti — And killing; yt — over the; prjā-pripālnṃ — enemies, through; yś — dharma; c — yuddha, and; sv-rājy-pripnthināṃ — those who; vdhḥ — are enemies; ayṃ — of our; rājyādhikṛtānāṃ — kingdom, have; kṣtriyāṇāṃ — to be; dhrmḥ — killed by; n — a Kshatriya; tu — in a; kṣtriy-mātrsy — dharma yuddha.; 3 — ## 4

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

vdhśc dhrmyuddhen svrājypripnthinām ||3||

Śrīdhara

nnu sv-dhrm-siddhye pṇḍitānām api rājy-kāṅkṣā yuktevety uktṃ ttrāh—kṣtriyāṇām iti | yt prjā-pripālnṃ yś c sv-rājy-pripnthināṃ vdhḥ, ayṃ rājyādhikṛtānāṃ kṣtriyāṇāṃ dhrmḥ, n tu kṣtriy-mātrsy ॥3॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

ननु स्व-धर्म-सिद्धये पण्डितानाम् अपि राज्य-काङ्क्षा युक्तेवेत्य् उक्तं तत्राह—क्षत्रियाणाम् इति । यत् प्रजा-परिपालनं यश् च स्व-राज्य-परिपन्थिनां वधः, अयं राज्याधिकृतानां क्षत्रियाणां धर्मः, न तु क्षत्रिय-मात्रस्य ॥३॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 3).

What you said is right, the duty of a Kshatriya is looking after the prajaas. And killing over the enemies, through dharma yuddha, and those who are enemies of our kingdom, have to be killed by a Kshatriya in a dharma yuddha. ## 4

VP 6.7.4

बन्धायैव भवत्येषा ह्यविद्याऽप्यक्रमोज्झिता

॥ Sri Engal Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

bndhāyaiv — ॥; bhvtyeṣā — Sri Engal; hyvidyā'pykrmojjhitā — Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Śrīdhara

ats — When; ttreti — I am; ttr — weak and; md-rājye — not capable; tvyāphṛte — of protecting; sti — my country,; punr — as I; ātm-sātkrtum — lost to; aśktsy — you, and; mm — you have; prjā-pripālnādi-tyāge'pi — taken over; doṣo — my kingdom,; naivāsti — and because; ttrāsktsyeti — of that,; pāṭhe — if I; ttr — am not; rājye — looking after; asktsy — my prajaas,; anāsktsy — that is; me — not a; doṣo — dosha for; nāstīty — me. I; arthḥ — have lost; rājyādhikārābhāven — the kingdom; td-adhikṛt-dhrme'py — to you.; adhikār-nivṛtteḥ — I do; anythā — not have; tu — the adhikaara; doṣḥ — to rule; syād — over now.; ev — So, there; n — is no; punr — dosha there.; avidyā — When adhikaara; asy — is there; krmṇs — for someone,; tyāge — even then,; kiṃ — if they; syād — leave the; iti — kingdom and; vktuṃ — do not; śkyṃ — look after; hi — the prajaas,; ysmād — then it; eṣā — will be; krm-myī — a defect; avidyāpi — on the; akrmojjhitā — part of; nyāyen — the king.; sty — Whereas I; evādhikāre — do not; prityktā — have the; stī — adhikaara. That; bndhāy — is why; bhvty — there is; evety — no dosha; arthḥ — for me.; 4 — ## 5

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

bndhāyaiv bhvtyeṣā hyvidyā'pykrmojjhitā ||4||

Śrīdhara

ats ttreti | ttr md-rājye tvyāphṛte sti punr ātm-sātkrtum aśktsy mm prjā-pripālnādi-tyāge'pi doṣo naivāsti | ttrāsktsyeti pāṭhe ttr rājye asktsy anāsktsy me doṣo nāstīty arthḥ | rājyādhikārābhāven td-adhikṛt-dhrme'py adhikār-nivṛtteḥ, anythā tu doṣḥ syād ev n punr avidyā | asy krmṇs tyāge kiṃ syād iti vktuṃ śkyṃ hi ysmād eṣā krm-myī avidyāpi akrmojjhitā nyāyen sty evādhikāre prityktā stī bndhāy bhvty evety arthḥ ॥4॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

अतस् तत्रेति । तत्र मद्-राज्ये त्वयापहृते सति पुनर् आत्म-सात्कर्तुम् अशक्तस्य मम प्रजा-परिपालनादि-त्यागेऽपि दोषो नैवास्ति । तत्रासक्तस्येति पाठे तत्र राज्ये असक्तस्य अनासक्तस्य मे दोषो नास्तीत्य् अर्थः । राज्याधिकाराभावेन तद्-अधिकृत-धर्मेऽप्य् अधिकार-निवृत्तेः, अन्यथा तु दोषः स्याद् एव न पुनर् अविद्या । अस्य कर्मणस् त्यागे किं स्याद् इति वक्तुं शक्यं हि यस्माद् एषा कर्म-मयी अविद्यापि अक्रमोज्झिता नयायेन सत्य् एवाधिकारे परित्यक्ता सती बन्धाय भवत्य् एवेत्य् अर्थः ॥४॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 4).

When I am weak and not capable of protecting my country, as I lost to you, and you have taken over my kingdom, and because of that, if I am not looking after my prajaas, that is not a dosha for me. I have lost the kingdom to you. I do not have the adhikaara to rule over now. So, there is no dosha there. When adhikaara is there for someone, even then, if they leave the kingdom and do not look after the prajaas, then it will be a defect on the part of the king. Whereas I do not have the adhikaara. That is why there is no dosha for me. ## 5

VP 6.7.5

अन्येषां दोषजा सैव धर्मं वै नानुरुध्यते

॥ Sri Engal Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

anyeṣāṃ — ॥; doṣjā — ॥; saiv — Sri; dhrmṃ — Engal; vai — Aalwaan’s; nānurudhyte — Commentary:

Śrīdhara

evṃ — When others; c — have got; sti — all the; mm — defects, it; vā — is all; mntriṇāṃ — because of; vā — their desire; tvd-rājye — and other; spṛhā — things which; dhrmāy — are not; n — correct. Because; bhvtīty — of the; āhjnmeti — defects of; ydy — that nature,; evṃ — they desire; dhrmḥ — to rule; tdā — over a; mmeyṃ — kingdom. That; rājy-spṛhā — is not; rāj-dhrmocit-cchtr-cāmrādi-jnmāntropbhog-lipsārthm — a proper; ev — dharma. It; bhvti — will only lead; n — to very; tu — meagre enjoyment; dhrmārthm — of bhogaas.; anyeṣāṃ — I am; scivādīnāṃ — not like; saiv — that. For; tvd-rājy-spṛhā — me, it; rāgādi-lobh-doṣjā — is not; ato — dharma. It; dhrmm — is only; ev — with the; kevlṃ — desire to; nānurdhyte — enjoy all; nānuvrtte — the things; api — here, which; tu — are very; dhrm-vñcnenārth-śāstrm — meagre, and; evānuvrtte — it will; ity — not be; arthḥ — dharma for; 5 — me. ## 6

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

anyeṣāṃ doṣjā saiv dhrmṃ vai nānurudhyte ||5||

Śrīdhara

evṃ c sti mm vā mntriṇāṃ vā tvd-rājye spṛhā dhrmāy n bhvtīty āh—jnmeti | ydy evṃ dhrmḥ, tdā mmeyṃ rājy-spṛhā rāj-dhrmocit-cchtr-cāmrādi-jnmāntropbhog-lipsārthm ev bhvti, n tu dhrmārthm | anyeṣāṃ scivādīnāṃ saiv tvd-rājy-spṛhā rāgādi-lobh-doṣjā, ato dhrmm ev kevlṃ nānurdhyte nānuvrtte, api tu dhrm-vñcnenārth-śāstrm evānuvrtte ity arthḥ ॥5॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

एवं च सति मम वा मन्त्रिणां वा त्वद्-राज्ये स्पृहा धर्माय न भवतीत्य् आह—जन्मेति । यद्य् एवं धर्मः, तदा ममेयं राज्य-स्पृहा राज-धर्मोचित-च्छत्र-चामरादि-जन्मान्तरोपभोग-लिप्सार्थम् एव भवति, न तु धर्मार्थम् । अन्येषां सचिवादीनां सैव त्वद्-राज्य-स्पृहा रागादि-लोभ-दोषजा, अतो धर्मम् एव केवलं नानुर्ध्यते नानुवर्तते, अपि तु धर्म-वञ्चनेनार्थ-शास्त्रम् एवानुवर्तते इत्य् अर्थः ॥५॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 5).

When others have got all the defects, it is all because of their desire and other things which are not correct. Because of the defects of that nature, they desire to rule over a kingdom. That is not a proper dharma. It will only lead to very meagre enjoyment of bhogaas. I am not like that. For me, it is not dharma. It is only with the desire to enjoy all the things here, which are very meagre, and it will not be dharma for me. ## 6

VP 6.7.5

अन्येषां दोषजा सैव धर्मं वै नानुरुध्यते

Translation cross-reference is supplied by the remote Vasuki English witness.

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

anyeṣāṃ — Translation; doṣjā — cross-reference is; saiv — supplied by; dhrmṃ — the; vai — remote Vasuki; nānurudhyte — English witness.

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

anyeṣāṃ doṣjā saiv dhrmṃ vai nānurudhyte ||5||

Śrīdhara commentary.

No commentary.

VP 6.7.6

अतो न याचितं राज्यमविद्यान्तर्गतं तव

॥ Sri Engal Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

ato — ॥; n — Sri; yācitṃ — Engal; rājymvidyāntrgtṃ — Aalwaan’s; tv — Commentary:

Śrīdhara

nnu — What Keshidhvaja wanted; tthāpi — to test was -; yācnā-mātreṇ — in order to; rājyṃ — rule over the prajaas,; lbdhvā — you have to; mhān — request for a kingdom,; dhrmḥ — you have to; kim — do yaachanaa, you have; iti — to beg. I; n — am not supposed to; kriyte — beg. Superior Kshatriyaas; ttrāhn — do not beg, even; yācñeti — if they want; ato — to rule over a; dhrmtvād — kingdom. I will; avidyāntrgttvāc — not beg. A shreshtha; c — Kshatriya thinks that; tvdīyṃ — one should not beg; rājyṃ — for a kingdom.; myā — Ruling over this kingdom,; n — and all of; prārthitm — that, I did not; 6 — ask you. ## 7

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

ato n yācitṃ rājymvidyāntrgtṃ tv ||6||

Śrīdhara

nnu tthāpi yācnā-mātreṇ rājyṃ lbdhvā mhān dhrmḥ kim iti n kriyte, ttrāh—n yācñeti | ato dhrmtvād avidyāntrgttvāc c tvdīyṃ rājyṃ myā n prārthitm ॥6॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

ननु तथापि याचना-मात्रेण राज्यं लब्ध्वा महान् धर्मः किम् इति न क्रियते, तत्राह—न याच्ञेति । अतो धर्मत्वाद् अविद्यान्तर्गतत्वाच् च त्वदीयं राज्यं मया न प्रार्थितम् ॥६॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 6).

What Keshidhvaja wanted to test was - in order to rule over the prajaas, you have to request for a kingdom, you have to do yaachanaa, you have to beg. I am not supposed to beg. Superior Kshatriyaas do not beg, even if they want to rule over a kingdom. I will not beg. A shreshtha Kshatriya thinks that one should not beg for a kingdom. Ruling over this kingdom, and all of that, I did not ask you. ## 7

VP 6.7.7

अहंमानमहापानमदमत्ता न मादृशाः

॥ Sri Engal Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Viṣṇu Purāṇa

Śrīdhara

ato — What was; ytr — told earlier, that; gṛdhnnty — those who; apṇḍitā — do not have; iti — the viveka; yd — go and run; uktṃ — after a kingdom,; td — they are; ev — always after possession,; siddhm — wanting more; ity — and more. Śrīdhara commentary (source range 7).

What was told earlier, that those who do not have the viveka go and run after a kingdom, they are always after possession, wanting more and more. This is not correct, they are all avivekis. They are not wise. They are as though intoxicated with the drink of ahamkaara, and I am not like that. ## 8

VP 6.7.9

Viṣṇu Purāṇa

Śrīdhara

avidyā-mye'pi — Keshidhvaja - In order to; rājye — do my varnaashrama dharma, to; sv-prvṛttau — get rid of this praarabdha karma; tvt — which is obstructing the upaasanaa,; kārṇm — I do various yaagaas, and; āhahṃ — rule over my kingdom. Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

rājyṃ yāgāṃśc vividhānbhogaiḥ puṇykṣyṃ tthā ||9||

Śrīdhara

avidyā-mye'pi rājye sv-prvṛttau tvt kārṇm āh—ahṃ hīti | styṃ tvyoktm avidyāntrgttvād rājym anupādeym iti tc c mmāpi tulyṃ tthāpi aviyyā prjāpālnādi-krm-lkṣṇyā sttv-śuddhyā kām-krodhādi-lkṣṇṃ mṛtyuṃ sṃsār-hetuṃ trtum icchnn ahṃ rājyādi kromi | avidyyā mṛtyuṃ tīrtvā vidyyāmṛtm aśnute ity ādi śruteḥ ॥9॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

अविद्या-मयेऽपि राज्ये स्व-प्रवृत्तौ तवत् कारणम् आह—अहं हीति । सत्यं त्वयोक्तम् अविद्यान्तर्गतत्वाद् राज्यम् अनुपादेयम् इति तच् च ममापि तुल्यं तथापि अवियया प्रजापालनादि-कर्म-लक्षणया सत्त्व-शुद्ध्या काम-क्रोधादि-लक्षणं मृत्युं संसार-हेतुं तर्तुम् इच्छन्न् अहं राज्यादि करोमि । अविद्यया मृत्युं तीर्त्वा विद्ययामृतम् अश्नुते इत्य् आदि श्रुतेः ॥९॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 9).

Keshidhvaja - In order to do my varnaashrama dharma, to get rid of this praarabdha karma which is obstructing the upaasanaa, I do various yaagaas, and rule over my kingdom. So, this is my varna dharma. With all this, I will get rid of my praarabdha karma, and my paapa, punya, and everything. Whatever punya I have accumulated, that has to be experienced also, all the bhogaas, enjoyments have to be done without any attachment; then that will also get exhausted. I am doing that. In this shloka, in order to tell the yoga svaroopa, and what is necessary as a part of that yoga svaroopa, in order to do upaasanaa, bhakti yoga, one has to know the aatma svaroopa jnyaana, aatma saakshaatkaara is also required, one should know the difference and distinction between prakruti and aatma, purusha, and then have aatma saakshaatkaara, and then go and do bhakti yoga. Shruti tells about aatma saakshaatkaara. Avidyayaa mrutyum teertvaa vidyayaa amrutam ashnute is told in Eeshaavaasya Upanishad. By performing the varnaashrama vihita karma, the praarabdha karma which is obstructing the start of upaasanaa, one can get rid of that, and then upaasanaa can be started. So, one has to perform varnaashrama dharma properly. ## 10

The; idṃ — wealth of; mnḥ — a wise; viveke — person should; aiśvryṃ — be the; sāmrthyṃ — viveka jnyaana; ysy — itself, they; tsy — should be; bhāvo — able to; vivekaiśvrytā — differentiate between; tāṃ — what is; gtṃ — right and; prāptm — wrong. tcchrūytāmvidyāyāḥsvrūpṃ kulnndn ||10||

Śrīdhara

tv puns td idṃ mnḥ viveke aiśvryṃ sāmrthyṃ ysy tsy bhāvo vivekaiśvrytā tāṃ gtṃ prāptm updeśādy-anpekṣṃ svym ev vivekādhikārtāṃ gtṃ prāptm etd diṣṭyā bhdrm ity arthḥ | vivekaiśvrym āgtm iti tu pāṭhḥ sugmḥ | td evṃ śiṣysy vivekādikṃ prīkṣyābhinndy edānīṃ ten yt pṛṣṭṃ tt kleś-prśmāyālṃ yt krm td udīryeti td vktuṃ prthmṃ tāvd avidyākhyṃ kleś-svrūpṃ drśyti—tc chrūytām ity ādi sārdhen śloken ॥10॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

तव पुनस् तद् इदं मनः विवेके ऐश्वर्यं सामर्थ्यं यस्य तस्य भावो विवेकैश्वर्यता तां गतं प्राप्तम् उपदेशाद्य्-अनपेक्षं स्वयम् एव विवेकाधिकारतां गतं प्राप्तम् एतद् दिष्ट्या भद्रम् इत्य् अर्थः । विवेकैश्वर्यम् आगतम् इति तु पाठः सुगमः । तद् एवं शिष्यस्य विवेकादिकं परीक्ष्याभिनन्द्य एदानीं तेन यत् पृष्टं तत् क्लेश-प्रशमायालं यत् कर्म तद् उदीरयेति तद् वक्तुं प्रथमं तावद् अविद्याख्यं क्लेश-स्वरूपं दर्शयति—तच् छ्रूयताम् इत्य् आदि सार्धेन श्लोकेन ॥१०॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 10).

Viveka itself is aishwarya. The wealth of a wise person should be the viveka jnyaana itself, they should be able to differentiate between what is right and wrong. You are fortunate that your mind is now getting this viveka, of what is right and wrong. You did not desire for the kingdom, but are asking me to teach this adhyaatma vijnyaana, so you are a viveki. You know how to differentiate between what is right and wrong. You are very fortunate to have such a mind. Listen to me about the svaroopa of avidyaa, I am going to teach you now. Avidyaa means the varnaashrama dharma, which I am going to teach you its nature. ## 11

VP 6.7.11

संसारतरुसंभूतिबीजमेतद्द्विधा स्थितम्

॥ Sri Engal Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

sṃsārtrusṃbhūtibījmetddvidhā — ॥ Sri; sthitm — Engal Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Śrīdhara

anātmni — This is a very important; dehādau — shloka which says what are the; sv-kṣetrādau — two main causes for samsaara. These; c — are the two causes for the; ahṃ-mmeti — seeds for the tree of samsaara.; yā — In order for the tree of; mtiḥ — samsaara to grow, these two are; sā — like the seeds for that.; avidyā — (1) That which is not aatma,; vipryy-rūpā — thinking that it is aatma. Deha; yā — is not aatma, it is anaatma,; anityāśuci-duḥkhānātmsu — thinking that deha is aatma, this; nity-śuci-sukhātm-khyātir — is dehaatma bhramaa. (2) That which; avidyā — is required to nurture and grow; yosū — the body, that is what; 25 — is required for me, if one; iti — thinks like that, they will only; pātñjlm — look after their body, and not; tsyā — their aatma. Thinking that it belongs; anrth-hetutvm — to me. Kaamya karmaas which are; āhetd — having some fruits, for the enjoyment; dvidhā — of the individual self, performing; drśitm — those, they get impermanent fruits, and; avidyā-svrūpṃ — benefits which will not last long.; sṃsār-tru-smbhūteḥ — This will only make one to; bījtyā — be more and more in samsaara; sthitm — only. This is the cause of; 11 — the tree of samsaara. ## 12

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

sṃsārtrusṃbhūtibījmetddvidhā sthitm ||11||

Śrīdhara

anātmni dehādau sv-kṣetrādau c ahṃ-mmeti yā mtiḥ sā avidyā vipryy-rūpā yā, anityāśuci-duḥkhānātmsu nity-śuci-sukhātm-khyātir avidyā [yo.sū. 2.5] iti pātñjlm | tsyā anrth-hetutvm āh—etd dvidhā drśitm avidyā-svrūpṃ sṃsār-tru-smbhūteḥ bījtyā sthitm ॥11॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

अनात्मनि देहादौ स्व-क्षेत्रादौ च अहं-ममेति या मतिः सा अविद्या विपर्यय-रूपा या, अनित्याशुचि-दुःखानात्मसु नित्य-शुचि-सुखात्म-ख्यातिर् अविद्या [यो.सू. २.५] इति पातञ्जलम् । तस्या अनर्थ-हेतुत्वम् आह—एतद् द्विधा दर्शितम् अविद्या-स्वरूपं संसार-तरु-सम्भूतेः बीजतया स्थितम् ॥११॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 11).

This is a very important shloka which says what are the two main causes for samsaara. These are the two causes for the seeds for the tree of samsaara. In order for the tree of samsaara to grow, these two are like the seeds for that. (1) That which is not aatma, thinking that it is aatma. Deha is not aatma, it is anaatma, thinking that deha is aatma, this is dehaatma bhramaa. (2) That which is required to nurture and grow the body, that is what is required for me, if one thinks like that, they will only look after their body, and not their aatma. Thinking that it belongs to me. Kaamya karmaas which are having some fruits, for the enjoyment of the individual self, performing those, they get impermanent fruits, and benefits which will not last long. This will only make one to be more and more in samsaara only. This is the cause of the tree of samsaara. ## 12

VP 6.7.11

VP 6.7.12

अहं ममैतदित्युच्चैः कुरुते कुमतिर्मतिम्

॥ Sri Engal Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

ahṃ — ॥; mmaitdityuccaiḥ — Sri; kurute — Engal; kumtirmtim — Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Śrīdhara

dehādāv — Ahamkaara and mamakaara, thinking that; ahṃ-mānsyāvidyātvm — what is not mine is mine,; uppādytipñceti — all the things needed to look; pñc-bhūtātmke — after the body, thinking that they; dehe — are all belonging to me, near; dehī — to me. This body which is; kumtiḥ — made of pancha bhootaas, this body; vipryy-mtiḥ — belongs to me, it is mine,; ahṃ — he thinks wrongly. Four shlokaas explain; mmaitd — this ahamkaara and mamakaara. This dehi,; iti — who is the aatman, this individual; mtiṃ — self, thinks that this panchabhootaatmaka shareera,; kurute — body made up of five; dvitīy-mti-śbds — elements, thinks that the body is; td-āvṛtty-arthḥ — mine, that I am the body.; vipryāse — He has the idea that he; hetuḥmohtmsā — is the aatman, in the body,; svrūpājñānenāvṛt — the dehaatma bhramaa. He thinks that; iti — the land and other things, that; ajñāte — they all belong to him, that; hi — he is the owner, that he; vstu-svrūpe — owns this, and this is all; vipryāso — mine. He is covered by poorva; bhvti — karma vaasanaa, the impressions which have; 12 — come from earlier births. ## 13

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

ahṃ mmaitdityuccaiḥ kurute kumtirmtim ||12||

Śrīdhara

dehādāv ahṃ-mānsyāvidyātvm uppādyti—pñceti | pñc-bhūtātmke dehe dehī kumtiḥ vipryy-mtiḥ ahṃ mmaitd iti mtiṃ kurute | dvitīy-mti-śbds td-āvṛtty-arthḥ | vipryāse hetuḥ—mohtmsā svrūpājñānenāvṛt iti | ajñāte hi vstu-svrūpe vipryāso bhvti ॥12॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

देहादाव् अहं-मानस्याविद्यात्वम् उपपादयति—पञ्चेति । पञ्च-भूतात्मके देहे देही कुमतिः विपर्यय-मतिः अहं ममैतद् इति मतिं कुरुते । द्वितीय-मति-शब्दस् तद्-आवृत्त्य्-अर्थः । विपर्यासे हेतुः—मोहतमसा स्वरूपाज्ञानेनावृत इति । अज्ञाते हि वस्तु-स्वरूपे विपर्यासो भवति ॥१२॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 12).

Ahamkaara and mamakaara, thinking that what is not mine is mine, all the things needed to look after the body, thinking that they are all belonging to me, near to me. This body which is made of pancha bhootaas, this body belongs to me, it is mine, he thinks wrongly. Four shlokaas explain this ahamkaara and mamakaara. This dehi, who is the aatman, this individual self, thinks that this panchabhootaatmaka shareera, body made up of five elements, thinks that the body is mine, that I am the body. He has the idea that he is the aatman, in the body, the dehaatma bhramaa. He thinks that the land and other things, that they all belong to him, that he is the owner, that he owns this, and this is all mine. He is covered by poorva karma vaasanaa, the impressions which have come from earlier births. ## 13

VP 6.7.13

आत्मन्यात्ममयं भावं कः करोति कलेवरे

Who can think that the body is aatman? This body is made of pancha bhootaas, five elements, so it is separate from the aatman. Who can even think that the aatman which is different from the kalebara, body; how can he think that the body itself is the aatman? Aatman is not the deha.

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

ātmnyātmmyṃ — Who can think that the body is aatman? This body; bhāvṃ — is made of pancha bhootaas, five elements, so it is separate; kḥ — from the aatman. Who can even think that the aatman which; kroti — is different from the kalebara, body; how can he think that; klevre — the body itself is the aatman? Aatman is not the deha.

Śrīdhara

kumtitvm — Who can think; ev — that the body is; spṣṭytiākāśeti — aatman? This body; ākāśādibhys — is made of pancha; tt-sākṣītyā — bhootaas, five elements,; ātmni — so it is separate; pṛthk — from the aatman.; sthite — Who can even think; sti — that the aatman; tt-kārye — which is different from; dehe — the kalebara, body;; ātm-myṃ — how can he think; bhāvṃ — that the body; kḥ — itself is the aatman?; kuryāt — Aatman is not; 13 — the deha. ## 14

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

ātmnyātmmyṃ bhāvṃ kḥ kroti klevre ||13||

Śrīdhara

kumtitvm ev spṣṭyti—ākāśeti | ākāśādibhys tt-sākṣītyā ātmni pṛthk sthite sti tt-kārye dehe ātm-myṃ bhāvṃ kḥ kuryāt ? ॥13॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

कुमतित्वम् एव स्पष्टयति—आकाशेति । आकाशादिभ्यस् तत्-साक्षीतया आत्मनि पृथक् स्थिते सति तत्-कार्ये देहे आत्म-मयं भावं कः कुर्यात् ? ॥१३॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 13).

Who can think that the body is aatman? This body is made of pancha bhootaas, five elements, so it is separate from the aatman. Who can even think that the aatman which is different from the kalebara, body; how can he think that the body itself is the aatman? Aatman is not the deha. ## 14

VP 6.7.15

करोति पण्डितःस्वाम्यमनात्मनि कलेवरे

॥ Sri Engal Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

kroti — ॥; pṇḍitḥsvāmymnātmni — Sri Engal; klevre — Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Śrīdhara

klevre — Kalebara, body is different from the aatman. The son, grandson, and others, who; anātmni — are all born of the body only, how can a knowledgeable, wise person; sti — think that these are all mine? All these are related only to the; td-utpāditeṣu — body, the sambandha is shareera sambandha, it is not aatma sambandha. The relationship of; kḥ — son, grandson, are not there in the aatma svaroopa; they are all because; svāmyṃ — of the body, which is associated with prakruti. How can someone think that; kroti — he is the owner of this, the master of this, that this thing; 15 — belongs to him, etc., when the body is different from the aatman? ## 16

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

kroti pṇḍitḥsvāmymnātmni klevre ||15||

Śrīdhara

klevre anātmni sti td-utpāditeṣu kḥ svāmyṃ kroti ? ॥15॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

कलेवरे अनात्मनि सति तद्-उत्पादितेषु कः स्वाम्यं करोति ? ॥१५॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 15).

Kalebara, body is different from the aatman. The son, grandson, and others, who are all born of the body only, how can a knowledgeable, wise person think that these are all mine? All these are related only to the body, the sambandha is shareera sambandha, it is not aatma sambandha. The relationship of son, grandson, are not there in the aatma svaroopa; they are all because of the body, which is associated with prakruti. How can someone think that he is the owner of this, the master of this, that this thing belongs to him, etc., when the body is different from the aatman? ## 16

VP 6.7.17

पार्थिवोऽयं तथा देहो मृदंब्वालेपनस्थितः

॥ Sri Engal Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

pārthivo'yṃ — ॥; tthā — Sri; deho — Engal; mṛdṃbvālepnsthitḥ — Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Śrīdhara

dehopbhogāyeti — Mixing water and; yd — mud, the house; uktṃ — is smeared and; td — made of mud only.; ev — Similarly, this body; dṛṣṭānten — is also paarthiva,; smrthyti — a modification of; mṛṇmym — pruthivi only, and it; ity — is also made; ādi — of mud and; dvābhyām — water. Here, out; mṛd-ambubhyām — of five, only pruthivi; ann-pān-rūpābhyām — is told here,; ālepnen — there are different; sthitir — opinions like that.; ysy — After pancheekarana, pruthivi itself; s — has all the; tthā — five elements in; 17 — different proportions. ## 18

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

pārthivo'yṃ tthā deho mṛdṃbvālepnsthitḥ ||17||

Śrīdhara

dehopbhogāyeti yd uktṃ td ev dṛṣṭānten smrthyti mṛṇmym ity ādi dvābhyām | mṛd-ambubhyām ann-pān-rūpābhyām ālepnen sthitir ysy s tthā ॥17॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

देहोपभोगायेति यद् उक्तं तद् एव दृष्टान्तेन समर्थयति मृण्मयम् इत्य् आदि द्वाभ्याम् । मृद्-अम्बुभ्याम् अन्न-पान-रूपाभ्याम् आलेपनेन स्थितिर् यस्य स तथा ॥१७॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 17).

Mixing water and mud, the house is smeared and made of mud only. Similarly, this body is also paarthiva, a modification of pruthivi only, and it is also made of mud and water. Here, out of five, only pruthivi is told here, there are different opinions like that. After pancheekarana, pruthivi itself has all the five elements in different proportions. ## 18

VP 6.7.18

आप्यायते यदि ततः पुंसो भोगोऽत्र किं कृतः

All the enjoyments are also panchabhootaatmaka only, the food that we eat, and others are all shabda sparsha roopa rasa gandha - all our enjoyments through the senses are all panchabhootaatmaka only. And the body is also panchabhootaatmaka. The body which is made of five elements is pleased with the objects of the senses which are also panchabhootaatmaka. What is this enjoyment for the aatman who is there, who is very different and distinct from the panchabhootaatmaka body? The next two shlokaas tell how this kind of a wrong understanding or ajnyaana can be got rid of.

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

āpyāyte — All the enjoyments are also panchabhootaatmaka only, the food that we eat, and; ydi — others are all shabda sparsha roopa rasa gandha - all our enjoyments through the; ttḥ — senses are all panchabhootaatmaka only. And the body is also panchabhootaatmaka. The body which; puṃso — is made of five elements is pleased with the objects of the senses which; bhogo'tr — are also panchabhootaatmaka. What is this enjoyment for the aatman who is there, who; kiṃ — is very different and distinct from the panchabhootaatmaka body? The next two shlokaas tell; kṛtḥ — how this kind of a wrong understanding or ajnyaana can be got rid of.

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

āpyāyte ydi ttḥ puṃso bhogo'tr kiṃ kṛtḥ ||18||

Śrīdhara commentary.

No commentary.

VP 6.7.19

मोहश्रमं प्रयातोऽसौ वासनारेणुकुण्ठितः

This is also an often quoted shloka. Thousands and thousands of births are coming in this path of samsaara, the cycle of births and deaths and births and deaths. He is extremely tired, and there is moha, because of the ignorance and wrong understanding, and all the delusion. He has the vaasanaas, the reminiscent impressions, from birth to birth to birth, where he is repeatedly doing some actions, and engaging in worldly enjoyments, related to body and senses, and of 194 Sri Vishnu Puraanam – Amsha 6 because of that, he has developed these vaasanaas, impressions, continuing from birth to birth. He is suffering great grief because of his ignorance. An individual self has taken thousands of births, and he is treading the path of samsaara, and he has got tremendous amount of grief, because of the ignorance, which is mohashrama, and because of the vaasanaas, which is ajnyaana karma ubhaya vaasanaa, which is ignorance, and karma, the reminiscent impressions getting passed on from birth to birth. And this is causing him all the grief and suffering. He has been suffering for innumerable births like this. This further continues, and there is description of aatma svaroopa, yoga. Which we see next. We are studying Chapter 7 of Amsha 6. Where Keshidhvaja is teaching the adhyaatma jnyaana that he has to Khaandikya Janaka. This starts with the teaching of the nature of the individual self, and the body, this tattva jnyaana, and then he goes on to teach the nature of the means, the yoga.

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

mohśrmṃ — This is also an often quoted shloka. Thousands and thousands of births are coming in this path of samsaara, the cycle of births and deaths and births and deaths. He is extremely tired, and there is moha, because of the ignorance and wrong understanding, and all the delusion. He has the vaasanaas, the reminiscent impressions, from birth to birth to birth, where he is repeatedly doing some actions, and engaging in worldly enjoyments, related to body and senses, and of 194 Sri Vishnu Puraanam; pryāto'sau — – Amsha 6 because of that, he has developed these vaasanaas, impressions, continuing from birth to birth. He is suffering great grief because of his ignorance. An individual self has taken thousands of births, and he is treading the path of samsaara, and he has got tremendous amount of grief, because of the ignorance, which is mohashrama, and because of the vaasanaas, which is ajnyaana karma ubhaya vaasanaa, which is ignorance, and karma, the reminiscent impressions getting passed on from birth to birth. And this; vāsnāreṇukuṇṭhitḥ — is causing him all the grief and suffering. He has been suffering for innumerable births like this. This further continues, and there is description of aatma svaroopa, yoga. Which we see next. We are studying Chapter 7 of Amsha 6. Where Keshidhvaja is teaching the adhyaatma jnyaana that he has to Khaandikya Janaka. This starts with the teaching of the nature of the individual self, and the body, this tattva jnyaana, and then he goes on to teach the nature of the means, the yoga.

Śrīdhara

VP 6.7.21

अनन्यातिशयाबाधं परं निर्वाणमृच्छति

When this ignorance is destroyed, when the grief is destroyed by proper knowledge, tattva jnyaana, the ignorance is dispelled, then he will be experiencing his real nature, he will get the aatma svaroopa jnyaana, which is incomparable, and there is nothing which can exceed this. This is the supreme of 194 Sri Vishnu Puraanam – Amsha 6 bliss he will experience. There is no grief at all there. He will experience the joy of supreme bliss. When he gets the proper knowledge, his ignorance is dispelled.

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

This is the supreme of 194 Sri Vishnu Puraanam – Amsha 6; nirvāṇmṛcchti — bliss he will experience. There is no grief at all there. He will experience the joy of supreme bliss. VP 6.7.24

भजते प्राकृतान् धर्मानन्यस्तेभ्यो हि सोऽव्ययः

Prakruti dharmaas means the association with prakruti. Because of this, he experiences grief which is not in his real nature. He is unchangeable, does not undergo any modification. In his real nature, he is pure. He is different from prakruti, as told here.

VP 6.7.27

यत्र स्थितो न च्यवते प्राप्य ब्रह्मलयं मुनिः

॥ Sri Engal Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

ytr — ॥; sthito — ॥; n — Sri; cyvte — Engal; prāpy — Engal; brhmlyṃ — Aalwaan’s; muniḥ — Commentary:

Śrīdhara

atr — Keshidhvaja - O Khaandikya, do listen to me, I am going to explain to you; yoge — the nature of yoga. One who is practicing yoga, will attain unity with Paramaatman, and; sthitḥ — he will never fall back from that, he will never come into this samsaara again.; ārūḍho — Brahma laya does not mean merging into Brahman. This does not mean losing one's identity.; muniḥ — There will be two substances only - jeevaatman and Paramaatman. Jeevaatman will not get destroyed.; brhm-lyṃ — It will be Brahmabhaava - jeevaatman will get similar characteristics as Brahman. His dharma bhoota; prāpy — jnyaana will be exactly same as Parabrahman. Even in Karma kaanda, the various karmaas are told;; n — when they perform the karmaas and worship Indra and other devataas, it is said that; cyvte — they go and merge with those devaas; this is not so; they go and attain; punr — their worlds and enjoy there. Or also as Prahlaada's experience, where he thought of himself; n — as Achyuta, he had that experience; he experienced the Parabrahman who was the inner self; amarti — of himself, this he addressed as 'Aham' , the deha vishishta aatma vishishta Paramaatman, the; 27 — Paramaatman who is aatman to him. This kind of experience is also meant here. ## 28

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

ytr sthito n cyvte prāpy brhmlyṃ muniḥ ||27||

Śrīdhara

atr yoge sthitḥ ārūḍho muniḥ brhm-lyṃ prāpy n cyvte punr n amarti ॥27॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

अत्र योगे स्थितः आरूढो मुनिः ब्रह्म-लयं प्राप्य न च्यवते पुनर् न अम्अरति ॥२७॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 27).

Keshidhvaja - O Khaandikya, do listen to me, I am going to explain to you the nature of yoga. One who is practicing yoga, will attain unity with Paramaatman, and he will never fall back from that, he will never come into this samsaara again. Brahma laya does not mean merging into Brahman. This does not mean losing one's identity. There will be two substances only - jeevaatman and Paramaatman. Jeevaatman will not get destroyed. It will be Brahmabhaava - jeevaatman will get similar characteristics as Brahman. His dharma bhoota jnyaana will be exactly same as Parabrahman. Even in Karma kaanda, the various karmaas are told; when they perform the karmaas and worship Indra and other devataas, it is said that they go and merge with those devaas; this is not so; they go and attain their worlds and enjoy there. Or also as Prahlaada's experience, where he thought of himself as Achyuta, he had that experience; he experienced the Parabrahman who was the inner self of himself, this he addressed as 'Aham' , the deha vishishta aatma vishishta Paramaatman, the Paramaatman who is aatman to him. This kind of experience is also meant here. ## 28

VP 6.7.29

चिन्तयेन्मुक्तये तेन ब्रह्मभूतं परेश्वरम्

॥ Sri Engal Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

cintyenmuktye — ॥; ten — Sri; brhmbhūtṃ — Engal; preśvrm — Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Śrīdhara

ato — One has to withdraw; mumukṣur — the mind from the; evṃ — sense objects. And concentrate; kuryād — it on the individual; ity — self. And then, he; āhviṣyebhy — has to meditate upon; iti — the Parabrahman for liberation.; vijñānātmā — He has to attain; vivek-jñān-yuktḥ — the distinctive knowledge among; pumān — prakruti, purusha and Eeshvara,; śbdādibhyo — the nature of individual; mnḥ — self, matter and Paramaatman,; prtyāhṛty — about how they are; ten — different and distinct from; mnā — each other. Then he; prm — has to meditate in; īśvrṃ — his mind, and the; cintyet — object of meditation should; 29 — be Parameshvara. ## 30

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

cintyenmuktye ten brhmbhūtṃ preśvrm ||29||

Śrīdhara

ato mumukṣur evṃ kuryād ity āh—viṣyebhy iti | vijñānātmā vivek-jñān-yuktḥ pumān śbdādibhyo mnḥ prtyāhṛty ten mnā prm īśvrṃ cintyet ॥29॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

अतो मुमुक्षुर् एवं कुर्याद् इत्य् आह—विषयेभ्य इति । विज्ञानात्मा विवेक-ज्ञान-युक्तः पुमान् शब्दादिभ्यो मनः प्रत्याहृत्य तेन मनआ परम् ईश्वरं चिन्तयेत् ॥२९॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 29).

One has to withdraw the mind from the sense objects. And concentrate it on the individual self. And then, he has to meditate upon the Parabrahman for liberation. He has to attain the distinctive knowledge among prakruti, purusha and Eeshvara, the nature of individual self, matter and Paramaatman, about how they are different and distinct from each other. Then he has to meditate in his mind, and the object of meditation should be Parameshvara. ## 30

VP 6.7.30

विकार्यमात्मनः शक्त्या लोहमाकर्षको यथा

॥ Sri Engal Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

vikārymātmnḥ — ॥; śktyā — Sri; lohmākrṣko — Engal; ythā — Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Śrīdhara

ttḥ — When a huge bundle of; kim — cotton is thrown into a fire,; at — it gets completely destroyed. In the; āhātm-bhāvm — same way, in a liberated; iti — one, the karma, vaasanaas, and all; cintitṃ — defects, paapaas are destroyed completely. All; td — these pramaanaas are giving similarity of; brhm — Paramaatman with Agni. Gold also; evṃ — becomes like fire as it shines,; dhyāyinṃ — burns like fire, when it comes; puruṣm — in contact with fire; when; ātm-bhāvm — all its impurities are removed, it; ātmaikyṃ — shines like fire. When gold is; nyti — impure, it will not shine forth.; vikāryṃ — When it is put into; vikārārhṃ — fire, all the impurities are removed,; lohṃ — and it shines forth like anything,; v-śktyā — and fire causes the gold to; ākrṣḥ — shine forth similar to itself.; aykānto — In the same way, when we; ythā — think about Parabrahman, when a yogi; ayṃ — meditates upon Parabrahman, his mind; tu — becomes pure and he gets liberated,; dṛṣṭāntḥ — and all his sins are destroyed; aṃyog-mātre — completely, his punya and paapa and; n — everything. And he attains Brahmabhaava.; tu — Paramaatman makes him to attain a; td-aikye — nature similar to him with respect; 30 — to his attributive consciousness. ## 31

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

vikārymātmnḥ śktyā lohmākrṣko ythā ||30||

Śrīdhara

ttḥ kim ? at āh—ātm-bhāvm iti | cintitṃ td brhm evṃ dhyāyinṃ puruṣm ātm-bhāvm ātmaikyṃ nyti vikāryṃ vikārārhṃ lohṃ v-śktyā ākrṣḥ aykānto ythā | ayṃ tu dṛṣṭāntḥ aṃyog-mātre, n tu td-aikye ॥30॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

ततः किम् ? अत आह—आत्म-भावम् इति । चिन्तितं तद् ब्रह्म एवं ध्यायिनं पुरुषम् आत्म-भावम् आत्मैक्यं नयति विकार्यं विकारार्हं लोहं व-शक्त्या आकर्षः अयकान्तो यथा । अयं तु दृष्टान्तः अंयोग-मात्रे, न तु तद्-ऐक्ये ॥३०॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 30).

When a huge bundle of cotton is thrown into a fire, it gets completely destroyed. In the same way, in a liberated one, the karma, vaasanaas, and all defects, paapaas are destroyed completely. All these pramaanaas are giving similarity of Paramaatman with Agni. Gold also becomes like fire as it shines, burns like fire, when it comes in contact with fire; when all its impurities are removed, it shines like fire. When gold is impure, it will not shine forth. When it is put into fire, all the impurities are removed, and it shines forth like anything, and fire causes the gold to shine forth similar to itself. In the same way, when we think about Parabrahman, when a yogi meditates upon Parabrahman, his mind becomes pure and he gets liberated, and all his sins are destroyed completely, his punya and paapa and everything. And he attains Brahmabhaava. Paramaatman makes him to attain a nature similar to him with respect to his attributive consciousness. ## 31

VP 6.7.31

तस्य ब्रह्मणि संयोगो योग इत्यभिधीयते

॥ Sri Engal Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

tsy — ॥; brhmṇi — Sri; sṃyogo — Engal; yog — Aalwaan’s; itybhidhīyte — Commentary:

Śrīdhara

idānīṃ — In these three-four shlokaas, the nature of yoga, the object of meditation,; yog-svrūpṃ — the fruit are all told. Now, is told in detail the nature of; drśytiātmeti — yoga, till the end of this chapter. From beginningless time, the self is; ātmnḥ — always associated with matter, and always after experience of the sense objects. The; prytno — impressions are continuing from birth to birth forever. And it is very difficult; ym-niymādi-viṣyḥ — to attain Paramaatman. One has to put a lot of effort to attain; tt-sāpekṣā — him. That requires the aatma prayatna, the efforts of the individual self, because; td-adhīnā — the individual self is always associated with karma, vaasanaa, etc. That meditation which; viśiṣṭā — is constant, continuous, and without any break, when it is done, leads to; sttv-myī — vivid perception of the Lord. It is of the nature of unparalleled bliss.; yā — Whatever are the stored defects of the mind, that mind has to be; mnso — associated with Brahman, should be focussed on Brahman. This is called yoga. There; vṛttiḥ — is another thought, where the meaning of yoga is laya, where aatma and; tyā — Paramaatman become one; there will be no two objects, only one will exist;; brhmṇy — this thought is refuted here, not accepted here. Because yoga is told as; ev — meditating upon the divya mangala vigraha of Brahman, which will be told later.; sṃyogo — One substance cannot become another substance. When the two are merged, the nature; yogḥ — of both will remain as it is. One cannot lose its identity completely,; 31 — there is no svaroopa naasha. So, laya is not accepted here. ## 32

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

tsy brhmṇi sṃyogo yog itybhidhīyte ||31||

Śrīdhara

idānīṃ yog-svrūpṃ drśyti—ātmeti | ātmnḥ prytno ym-niymādi-viṣyḥ, tt-sāpekṣā td-adhīnā viśiṣṭā sttv-myī yā mnso vṛttiḥ, tyā brhmṇy ev sṃyogo yogḥ ॥31॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

इदानीं योग-स्वरूपं दर्शयति—आत्मेति । आत्मनः प्रयत्नो यम-नियमादि-विषयः, तत्-सापेक्षा तद्-अधीना विशिष्टा सत्त्व-मयी या मनसो वृत्तिः, तया ब्रह्मण्य् एव संयोगो योगः ॥३१॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 31).

In these three-four shlokaas, the nature of yoga, the object of meditation, the fruit are all told. Now, is told in detail the nature of yoga, till the end of this chapter. From beginningless time, the self is always associated with matter, and always after experience of the sense objects. The impressions are continuing from birth to birth forever. And it is very difficult to attain Paramaatman. One has to put a lot of effort to attain him. That requires the aatma prayatna, the efforts of the individual self, because the individual self is always associated with karma, vaasanaa, etc. That meditation which is constant, continuous, and without any break, when it is done, leads to vivid perception of the Lord. It is of the nature of unparalleled bliss. Whatever are the stored defects of the mind, that mind has to be associated with Brahman, should be focussed on Brahman. This is called yoga. There is another thought, where the meaning of yoga is laya, where aatma and Paramaatman become one; there will be no two objects, only one will exist; this thought is refuted here, not accepted here. Because yoga is told as meditating upon the divya mangala vigraha of Brahman, which will be told later. One substance cannot become another substance. When the two are merged, the nature of both will remain as it is. One cannot lose its identity completely, there is no svaroopa naasha. So, laya is not accepted here. ## 32

VP 6.7.33

विनिष्पन्नसमाधिस्तु परं ब्रह्मोपलब्धिमान्

॥ Sri Engal Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

viniṣpnnsmādhistu — ॥; prṃ — Sri Engal; brhmoplbdhimān — Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Śrīdhara

idānīṃ — When one practices yoga, in; yogino'vthā-bheden — the initial stages, when still; traividhym — practicing yoga, he is said to; āhyog-yug — be a yogayuk. And one; iti — who has attained samaadhi, having; prthmṃ — passed through all the stages of; yog-niṣptteḥ — yoga, and attained the final; pūrvṃ — stage of beatitude, he will; td-arthṃ — attain Parambrahma, he is said to; yogābhyāṃ — be vinishpanna samaadhi. Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

viniṣpnnsmādhistu prṃ brhmoplbdhimān ||33||

Śrīdhara

idānīṃ yogino'vthā-bheden traividhym āh—yog-yug iti | prthmṃ yog-niṣptteḥ pūrvṃ td-arthṃ yogābhyāṃ kurvn yog-yug ity ucyte | prṃ ttḥ pścāt brhmoplbdhimān an viniṣpnn-smādhiḥ kthyte ॥33॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

इदानीं योगिनोऽवथा-भेदेन त्रैविध्यम् आह—योग-युग् इति । प्रथमं योग-निष्पत्तेः पूर्वं तद्-अर्थं योगाभ्यां कुर्वन् योग-युग् इत्य् उच्यते । परं ततः पश्चात् ब्रह्मोपलब्धिमान् अन् विनिष्पन्न-समाधिः कथ्यते ॥३३॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 33).

When one practices yoga, in the initial stages, when still practicing yoga, he is said to be a yogayuk. And one who has attained samaadhi, having passed through all the stages of yoga, and attained the final stage of beatitude, he will attain Parambrahma, he is said to be vinishpanna samaadhi. Based on the state in which a yogi is, the names are different. One who has started on yoga and is still progressing, is called yogayuk. One who has realized Paramaatman, is called vinishpanna samaadhi. Samaadhi comes after dhyaana. Up to dhyaana, the seven stages told - yama, niyama, aasana, praanaayaama, pratyaahaara, dhaaranaa, dhyaana - whoever is in all these stages is called a yogayuk. ## 34

जन्मान्तरैरभ्यसतो मुक्तिः पुर्वस्य जायते

The various obstructions which can come for yoga are all told here. He has to practice for many many births. Then he can move forward and get liberated.

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 34).

The various obstructions which can come for yoga are all told here. He has to practice for many many births. Then he can move forward and get liberated. ## 35

प्राप्नोति योगी योगाग्निदग्धकर्मचयोऽचिरात्

योगाग्नि-दग्ध कर्म-समूहः ॥३५॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 35).

Svaada means that they will have divya rasaasvaada, have divine taste. The divine experiences are told. तथा

VP 6.7.36

सेवेत योगी निष्कामो योग्यतां स्वमनो नयन्

॥ Sri Engal Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

sevet — ॥; yogī — ॥; niṣkāmo — Sri; yogytāṃ — Engal; svmno — Aalwaan’s; nyn — Commentary:

Śrīdhara

idānīṃ — The aatma gunaas, the discipline; ym-niymān-prāṇāyām-prtyāhār-dhārṇā-dhyān-smādhy — which a yogi has to; ity — practice are told here. Yama; aṣṭau — and Niyama are told like; yogāṅgāni — this. Ahimsaa, satya, asteya, brahmacharya,; krmeṇ — aparigraha, are Yama. Brahmacharya is; drśyiṣyn — not associating with women. Aparigraha; ym-niymm — is not possessing or accepting; āhbrhmcrym — things which are not allowed; ity-ādi — as per shaastra. Asteya is; tribhiḥ — not stealing. Satya is telling; svkīyṃ — the truth. Without desiring any; mno — other fruit, one has to; brhm-prveś-yogytāṃ — be devoted and performing yoga.; prāpyn — And his mind will attain; 36 — perfection in this way. ## 37

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

sevet yogī niṣkāmo yogytāṃ svmno nyn ||36||

Śrīdhara

idānīṃ ym-niymān-prāṇāyām-prtyāhār-dhārṇā-dhyān-smādhy ity aṣṭau yogāṅgāni krmeṇ drśyiṣyn ym-niymm āh—brhmcrym ity-ādi tribhiḥ | svkīyṃ mno brhm-prveś-yogytāṃ prāpyn ॥36॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

इदानीं यम-नियमान-प्राणायाम-प्रत्याहार-धारणा-ध्यान-समाधय इत्य् अष्टौ योगाङ्गानि क्रमेण दर्शयिष्यन् यम-नियमम् आह—ब्रह्मचर्यम् इत्य्-आदि त्रिभिः। स्वकीयं मनो ब्रह्म-प्रवेश-योग्यतां प्रापयन् ॥३६॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 36).

The aatma gunaas, the discipline which a yogi has to practice are told here. Yama and Niyama are told like this. Ahimsaa, satya, asteya, brahmacharya, aparigraha, are Yama. Brahmacharya is not associating with women. Aparigraha is not possessing or accepting things which are not allowed as per shaastra. Asteya is not stealing. Satya is telling the truth. Without desiring any other fruit, one has to be devoted and performing yoga. And his mind will attain perfection in this way. ## 37

VP 6.7.37

कुर्वीत ब्रह्मणि तथा परस्मिन्प्रवणं मनः

॥ Sri Engal Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

kurvīt — ॥; brhmṇi — Sri; tthā — Engal; prsminprvṇṃ — Aalwaan’s; mnḥ — Commentary:

Śrīdhara

prsmin — Niyama is told here. Svaadhyaaya is study; brhmṇi — of the Vedaas. Shoucha is shaastreeya shuddhi,; mnḥ — baahya and antara. Tapas is upavaasa and; prvṇṃ — others, and one who can tolerate thirst; kurvītety — and hunger, and practice mouna, and krucchra,; anen — chaandraayana. Whatever one possesses, not desiring for; īśvr-prṇidhānākhyṃ — anything more is called as santosha. Focussing; pñcmṃ — the mind on Parabrahman is also a; niymṃ — guna. The mind should be always focussed; drśyti — on attaining Paramaatman, with great devotion, meditating; 37-38 — on His divya mangala vigraha. ## 38

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

परस्मिन् ब्रह्मणि मनः प्रवणं कुर्वीतेत्य् अनेन ईश्वर-प्रणिधानाख्यं पञ्चमं नियमं दर्शयति ॥३७-३८॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 37–38).

Niyama is told here. Svaadhyaaya is study of the Vedaas. Shoucha is shaastreeya shuddhi, baahya and antara. Tapas is upavaasa and others, and one who can tolerate thirst and hunger, and practice mouna, and krucchra, chaandraayana. Whatever one possesses, not desiring for anything more is called as santosha. Focussing the mind on Parabrahman is also a guna. The mind should be always focussed on attaining Paramaatman, with great devotion, meditating on His divya mangala vigraha. ## 38

āsnm — Bhadraasana, Svastika, the; āhekm — various aasanaas are; iti — all told by; ymākhyair — Yaajnyavalkya. With; sn — Yama and Niyama,; bhdrāsnādīnāṃ — whatever was told, endowed; lkṣṇm — with all of; uktṃ — this, one should; yogi-yājñvlkīye — practice yoga and meditate; gulphau — upon Brahman. Śrīdhara Sanskrit

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 39).

Bhadraasana, Svastika, the various aasanaas are all told by Yaajnyavalkya. Endowed with all the qualities, one should sit in a proper aasana, sthiram sukham aasanam. With Yama and Niyama, whatever was told, endowed with all of this, one should practice yoga and meditate upon Brahman. Next, will be told about Praanaayaama. Which is explained in detail, which we see next. We are studying Chapter 7 of Amsha 6. Where Keshidhvaja is teaching the nature of yoga to Khaandikya Janaka. In this, he told about Yama, Niyama, Aasana, the initial three angaas of yoga. Now, he is going to teach about Praanaayaama and further stages. ## 40

॥ Sri Engal Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

kurutḥs — ॥; dvidhā — Sri; nen — Engal; tṛtīyḥsṃymāttyoḥ — Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Śrīdhara

dvividhsyāpi — This; ty — This; puns — This; traividhym — is; āhprspreṇeti — is; niśvāse — the; nāsikābhyāṃ — the; nirgcchti — practice; yo — practice; vāyuḥ — which; s — which; prāṇḥ — happens; ucchvāsenāntḥ — happens; prviśti — by; yḥ — by; so'pānḥ — clearing; ttr — clearing; prāṇ-vṛttyā — the; apān-vṛtter — the; abhibhvo — nose.; nirodho — nose.; reckākhyḥ — First,; prāṇāyāmḥ — First,; evm — the; apān-vṛttyā — the; prāṇ-vṛtter — nose; abhibhvḥ — nose; pūrkākhyḥ — is; evm — is; anen — to; prsprābhibhv-prkār-dvyen — to; s — be; prāṇāyāmo — be; dvidhā — cleared,; anyor — cleared,; yugpt — and; sṃymāt — and; kumbhkākhy — then; tṛtīyḥ — then; prāṇāyāmḥ — the; yd — the; vā — Praanaayaama; s — Praanaayaama; dvidhānenety — is; ekm — is; ev — to; pdṃ — to; ttr — be; cāym — be; arthḥ — done.; sd-vidhānen — done.; sd-gurūpdiṣṭ-mārgeṇ — Sveda,; reck-pūrkābhyāṃ — Sveda,; yt — Utkramana; prsprābhibhūtṃ — Utkramana; dvym — are; yś — are; c — told; kumbhkenobhyoḥ — told; shābhibhvḥ — in; evm — in; abhibhv-tryeṇaikḥ — Yogaanushaasana.; prāṇāyām — Yogaanushaasana.; iti — अर्भगश्च; 41 — अर्भगश्च

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

kurutḥs dvidhā nen tṛtīyḥsṃymāttyoḥ ||41||

Śrīdhara

dvividhsyāpi ty puns traividhym āh—prspreṇeti | niśvāse nāsikābhyāṃ nirgcchti yo vāyuḥ s prāṇḥ | ucchvāsenāntḥ prviśti yḥ, so'pānḥ | ttr prāṇ-vṛttyā apān-vṛtter abhibhvo nirodho reckākhyḥ prāṇāyāmḥ | evm apān-vṛttyā prāṇ-vṛtter abhibhvḥ pūrkākhyḥ | evm anen prsprābhibhv-prkār-dvyen s prāṇāyāmo dvidhā | anyor yugpt sṃymāt kumbhkākhy tṛtīyḥ prāṇāyāmḥ | yd vā, s dvidhānenety ekm ev pdṃ ttr cāym arthḥ | sd-vidhānen sd-gurūpdiṣṭ-mārgeṇ reck-pūrkābhyāṃ yt prsprābhibhūtṃ dvym | yś c kumbhkenobhyoḥ shābhibhvḥ | evm abhibhv-tryeṇaikḥ prāṇāyām iti ॥41॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

द्विविधस्यापि तय पुनस् त्रैविध्यम् आह—परस्परेणेति । निश्वासे नासिकाभ्यां निर्गच्छति यो वायुः स प्राणः । उच्छ्वासेनान्तः प्रविशति यः, सोऽपानः । तत्र प्राण-वृत्त्या अपान-वृत्तेर् अभिभवो निरोधो रेचकाख्यः प्राणायामः । एवम् अपान-वृत्त्या प्राण-वृत्तेर् अभिभवः पूरकाख्यः । एवम् अनेन परस्पराभिभव-प्रकार-द्वयेन स प्राणायामो द्विधा । अनयोर् युगपत् संयमात् कुम्भकाख्य तृतीयः प्राणायामः । यद् वा, स द्विधानेनेत्य् एकम् एव पदं तत्र चायम् अर्थः । सद्-विधानेन सद्-गुरूपदिष्ट-मार्गेण रेचक-पूरकाभ्यां यत् परस्पराभिभूतं द्वयम् । यश् च कुम्भकेनोभयोः सहाभिभवः । एवम् अभिभव-त्रयेणैकः प्राणायाम इति ॥४१॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 41).

This is the practice which happens by clearing the nose. First, the nose is to be cleared, and then the Praanaayaama is to be done. Sveda, Utkramana are told in Yogaanushaasana. अर्भगश्च

VP 6.7.43

कुर्याच्चित्तानुकारीणि प्रत्याहारपरायणः

॥ Sri Engal Aalwaan’s Commentary:

prtyāhārm — This is Pratyaahaara told here. These two; āhśbdādiṣv — shlokaas are telling about Pratyaahaara. प्रत्याहारम् आह—शब्दादिष्व् इति । चित्तानुचारीणि चित्तालम्बनाद् अन्यालम्बन-शून्यानीत्य् अर्थः ॥४३-४४॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 43–44).

This is Pratyaahaara told here. These two shlokaas are telling about Pratyaahaara. The indriyaas which are always going after shabda, sparsha, roopa, rasa, gandha, the sense objects, controlling them from that, and withdrawing them is called Pratyaahaara. One has to withdraw all the indriyaas, and make his mind steady and free from all these sense objects. ## 44

VP 6.7.44

इन्द्रियाणामवश्यैस्तैर्न योगी योगसाधकः

Their mind will be very steady, and they will be able to control their mind from going after the sense objects. Those who are not controlling the sense organs, they cannot practice yoga, they will not attain any siddhi in yoga. Those who do not have control on the senses cannot practice yoga. of 194 Sri Vishnu Puraanam – Amsha 6

Those who do not have control on the senses cannot practice yoga. of 194 Sri Vishnu Puraanam – Amsha 6

वशीकृते ततः कुर्यात्स्थितं चेतः शुभाश्रये

tsmāt — Controlling the praana with Praanaayaama, with; prāṇāyām-prtyāhār-pūrvkm — Pratyaahaara, controlling the indriyaas, the sense organs; ev — - controlling both of these, he has; dhārānām — to meditate upon the Shubhaashraya of Paramaatman,; abhysed — the divya mangala vigraha of Paramaatman. Śrīdhara commentary (source range 45).

Controlling the praana with Praanaayaama, with Pratyaahaara, controlling the indriyaas, the sense organs - controlling both of these, he has to meditate upon the Shubhaashraya of Paramaatman, the divya mangala vigraha of Paramaatman. In the mind, the sthoola roopa of Paramaatman has to be steadily contemplated upon. This is said to be Dhaaranaa. ## 46

VP 6.7.47

भूप मूर्तममूर्तं च परं चापरमेव च

॥ Sri Engal Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

bhūp — ॥; mūrtmmūrtṃ — ॥; c — Sri; prṃ — Engal; cāprmev — Aalwaan’s; c — Commentary:

Śrīdhara

tm — Keshidhvaja - The aashraya for the mind,; evāhāśry — the support for the mind, the mind has; iti — to have an object to think about,; cet — one has to meditate upon an object only.; āśryo — Without an object, one cannot do meditation.; brhmaiv — That aashraya for the mind, is of two; tc — types - the moorta and amoorta roopaas,; c — and para roopa and apara roopa. Shubhaashraya is to be; ythā-yogyṃ — told, before that, first, the ashubhaashraya is told,; mūrtāmūrt-prāpr-bheden — those which are not Shubhaashraya. The Baddha; cturdhāvsthitm — jeevaas, Mukta jeevaas are all not Shubhaashraya, as; krmeṇ — they are either ashubha, or they do; dhārṇe — not have aashraya. Only Bhagavaan's form is Shubhaashraya.; viṣye — Before telling the Bhagavaan's form, which is; iti — Shubhaashraya, the ashubhaashraya which are not fit for; drśyitum — meditation, are told now with moorta and; brhmṇś — amoorta roopaas. These are the Kshara and Akshara; cāturvidhym — told in Bhagavad Geeta - Ksharah sarvaani; āhtc — bhootaani kootastho akshara uchyate. And Para and Apara; c — are told. Moorta roopa is the Chaturmukha; brhm — Brahma and all the bound selves, who are; mūrtāmūrtṃ — in the prakruti mandala, and are having; ceti — a body; they are associated with matter, prakruti,; dvidhā — and they have a praakruta shareera; starting; vbhāvtḥ — from Chaturmukha down to all the beings in; vecchātḥ — this prakruti mandala, the bound selves. Amoorta; mūrtṃ — is without a shareera, the mukta roopa, the; mūrtimt — liberated selves. One is Para, and another; amūrtṃ — is Apara. How can they be told as; td-rhitṃ — Brahma dvidhaa? Because they are all Brahmaatmaka.; tt — Moorta roopaas and Amoorta roopaas are also that; punḥ — of Parabrahman only as they all have; prtyekṃ — Brahman only as their inner self. This Brahmaatmaka; prṃ — naanaatva is told in the Shrutis only.; cāprṃ — Abrahmaatmaka naanaatva is what is negated. Moorta and; ceti — Amoorta are said to be Brahman only,; dvidhā — as they are Brahmaatmaka. They are also forms; ttr — of Bhagavaan only, as told in Sarvam; prm-mūrtṃ — Khalvidam Brahma. But can they be meditated upon,; ṣḍguṇeśvr-rūpm — if a question arises, they are not; prṃ — fit for meditation. In order to tell this,; mūrtṃ — this has been told by Keshidhvaja initially.; pdmnābhādi-līlā-vigrh-rūpm — First, he tells about the baddha svaroopa, and; apr-mūrtṃ — the muktaas. He says that both are; hirṇygrbhādi-viśvrūpm — not fit for meditation. And then he tells; 47 — about Bhagavat roopa, which is Shubhaashraya. ## 48

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

bhūp mūrtmmūrtṃ c prṃ cāprmev c ||47||

Śrīdhara

tm evāh—āśry iti | cet āśryo brhmaiv, tc c mnd-mdhymottmādhikāriṇāṃ ythā-yogyṃ mūrtāmūrt-prāpr-bheden cturdhāvsthitm | krmeṇ dhārṇe viṣye iti drśyitum brhmṇś cāturvidhym āh—tc c brhm mūrtāmūrtṃ ceti dvidhā vbhāvtḥ vecchātḥ mūrtṃ mūrtimt amūrtṃ td-rhitṃ, tt punḥ prtyekṃ prṃ cāprṃ ceti dvidhā | ttr prm-mūrtṃ ṣḍguṇeśvr-rūpm | prṃ mūrtṃ pdmnābhādi-līlā-vigrh-rūpm | apr-mūrtṃ hirṇygrbhādi-viśvrūpm ॥47॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

तम् एवाह—आश्रय इति । चेत आश्रयो ब्रह्मैव, तच् च मन्द-मध्यमोत्तमाधिकारिणां यथा-योग्यं मूर्तामूर्त-परापर-भेदेन चतुर्धावस्थितम्। क्रमेण धारणे विषये इति दर्शयितुम् ब्रह्मणश् चातुर्विध्यम् आह—तच् च ब्रह्म मूर्तामूर्तं चेति द्विधा वभावतः वेच्छातः मूर्तं मूर्तिमत् अमूर्तं तद्-रहितं, तत् पुनः प्रत्येकं परं चापरं चेति द्विधा । तत्र परम-मूर्तं षड्गुणेश्वर-रूपम् । परं मूर्तं पद्मनाभादि-लीला-विग्रह-रूपम् । अपर-मूर्तं हिरण्यगर्भादि-विश्वरूपम् ॥४७॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 47).

Keshidhvaja - The aashraya for the mind, the support for the mind, the mind has to have an object to think about, one has to meditate upon an object only. Without an object, one cannot do meditation. That aashraya for the mind, is of two types - the moorta and amoorta roopaas, and para roopa and apara roopa. Initially, the ashubhaashraya is told. Shubhaashraya is to be told, before that, first, the ashubhaashraya is told, those which are not Shubhaashraya. The Baddha jeevaas, Mukta jeevaas are all not Shubhaashraya, as they are either ashubha, or they do not have aashraya. Only Bhagavaan's form is Shubhaashraya. Before telling the Bhagavaan's form, which is Shubhaashraya, the ashubhaashraya which are not fit for meditation, are told now with moorta and amoorta roopaas. These are the Kshara and Akshara told in Bhagavad Geeta - Ksharah sarvaani bhootaani kootastho akshara uchyate. And Para and Apara are told. Moorta roopa is the Chaturmukha Brahma and all the bound selves, who are in the prakruti mandala, and are having a body; they are associated with matter, prakruti, and they have a praakruta shareera; starting from Chaturmukha down to all the beings in this prakruti mandala, the bound selves. Amoorta is without a shareera, the mukta roopa, the liberated selves. One is Para, and another is Apara. How can they be told as Brahma dvidhaa? Because they are all Brahmaatmaka. Moorta roopaas and Amoorta roopaas are also that of Parabrahman only as they all have Brahman only as their inner self. This Brahmaatmaka naanaatva is told in the Shrutis only. Abrahmaatmaka naanaatva is what is negated. Moorta and Amoorta are said to be Brahman only, as they are Brahmaatmaka. They are also forms of Bhagavaan only, as told in Sarvam Khalvidam Brahma. But can they be meditated upon, if a question arises, they are not fit for meditation. In order to tell this, this has been told by Keshidhvaja initially. First, he tells about the baddha svaroopa, and the muktaas. He says that both are not fit for meditation. And then he tells about Bhagavat roopa, which is Shubhaashraya. ## 48

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 48).

VP 6.7.50

कर्मभावनया चान्ये देवाद्याः स्थावरावराः

॥ Sri Engal Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

krmbhāvnyā — ॥; cānye — Sri; devādyāḥ — Engal; sthāvrāvrāḥ — Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Śrīdhara

ttr — Śrīdhara; vyṃ — Śrīdhara; brhmaiv — explains; bhvāmḥ — the; vyṃ — preceding; krmaiv — passage; kurmḥ — in; vyṃ — relation; punr-ubhym — to; idm — the; anuanddhm — chapter’s; ity — teaching;; evṃ — the; trividh-bhāvnā-yuktān — exact; jīvān — Sanskrit; udāhrtianndnādy — note; iti — is; dvābhyām — preserved; 50 — above.

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

krmbhāvnyā cānye devādyāḥ sthāvrāvrāḥ ||50||

Śrīdhara

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

VP 6.7.51

बोधाधिकारयुक्तेषु विद्यते भावभावना

॥ Sri Engal Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

bodhādhikāryukteṣu — ॥; vidyte — Sri Engal; bhāvbhāvnā — Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Śrīdhara

bodhḥ — Adhikara is thinking that whatever karma one does, is; svrūp-viṣyḥ — for my purpose only, and for me to gain whatever; adhikārś — fruits I want. If one wants svarga phala, then this; c — karma is for me to attain this svarga phala.; srgādi-viṣyḥ — Like that, if one thinks that it is for them,; td-yukteṣu — and for attaining whatever desires they have, and this is; td-ubhyānusndhānātmikā — said to be adhikaara. Whoever in this samsaara dashaa, thinks; dvidhā — that the karmaas are for fulfilling various desires for; bhāvnā — them, are meant for them, is told here. Hiranyagarbha and; vidyte — others have both Brahmabhaavanaa and Karmabhaavanaa. Anyone performing karmaas for; 51 — their own purpose, they have all these bhaavanaas. ## 52

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

bodhādhikāryukteṣu vidyte bhāvbhāvnā ||51||

Śrīdhara

bodhḥ svrūp-viṣyḥ | adhikārś c srgādi-viṣyḥ, td-yukteṣu td-ubhyānusndhānātmikā dvidhā bhāvnā vidyte ॥51॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

बोधः स्वरूप-विषयः । अधिकारश् च सर्गादि-विषयः, तद्-युक्तेषु तद्-उभयानुसन्धानात्मिका द्विधा भावना विद्यते ॥५१॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 51).

Adhikara is thinking that whatever karma one does, is for my purpose only, and for me to gain whatever fruits I want. If one wants svarga phala, then this karma is for me to attain this svarga phala. Like that, if one thinks that it is for them, and for attaining whatever desires they have, and this is said to be adhikaara. Whoever in this samsaara dashaa, thinks that the karmaas are for fulfilling various desires for them, are meant for them, is told here. Hiranyagarbha and others have both Brahmabhaavanaa and Karmabhaavanaa. Anyone performing karmaas for their own purpose, they have all these bhaavanaas. ## 52

VP 6.7.55

ततःस्थूलं हरे रूपं चिन्तयेद्विश्वगोचरम्

॥ Sri Engal Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

ttḥsthūlṃ — ॥; hre — Sri; rūpṃ — Engal; cintyedviśvgocrm — Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Śrīdhara

nnu — Yogayuk is one who is; trhi — in any of the stages; śreṣṭhtvād — of yoga still. He has; idm — started yoga, and has not; ev — yet reached the final stage.; cintyituṃ — One who has reached the; yuktm — final stage is said to; ttrāhneti — be a nishpanna yogi. For; yog-yujā — a yogayuk, it is not; prthmābhyāsinā — possible to meditate upon the; 55 — form of the Lord. ## 56

Transliteration

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

ननु तर्हि श्रेष्ठत्वाद् इदम् एव चिन्तयितुं युक्तम् ? तत्राह—नेति योग-युजा प्रथमाभ्यासिना ॥५५॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 55).

Yogayuk is one who is in any of the stages of yoga still. He has started yoga, and has not yet reached the final stage. One who has reached the final stage is said to be a nishpanna yogi. For a yogayuk, it is not possible to meditate upon the form of the Lord. ## 56

VP 6.7.60

परब्रह्मस्वरूपस्य विष्णोः शक्तिसमन्वितम्

॥ Sri Engal Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

prbrhmsvrūpsy — ॥; viṣṇoḥ — Sri Engal; śktismnvitm — Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Śrīdhara

etc — ... all of these which have the trividha bhaavanaas, brahma bhaavana, karma bhaavana, ubhaya; c — bhaavana, this gross form of Paramaatman, though it can be meditated upon as it; tttvto — has a gross form, it has this bhaavanaa traya. Because of that, it is; viṣṇoḥ — ashuddha, it is impure and not fit for meditation, it is not said to; svrūpm — be Shubhaashraya, it is ashubhaashraya. Idam vishvam is the conclusion of whatever is told; iti — in this world. From Jagadetat charaacharam is a separate sentence. This is all said; cintnīyṃ — to be the shakti of Vishnu only. Shakti, amsha, tanu, vapu, shareera - all; kintu — of these are paryaaya padaas, synonyms. This is controlled by Paramaatman, supported by Him,; tc-chkti-vyāptm — and meant for His purpose. He is the master of that. It is all; ity — shakti because it is supported by His sankalpa shakti. Just like our shareera, as; āhśkti-smnvitm — our Jeevaatman is pervading the whole body, and is controlling by his willing only.; iti — In the same way, Paramaatman controls the whole world of sentients and non-sentients. There; 60 — is another way of commenting on this, as told by some people. ## 61

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

prbrhmsvrūpsy viṣṇoḥ śktismnvitm ||60||

Śrīdhara

etc c tttvto viṣṇoḥ svrūpm iti cintnīyṃ, kintu tc-chkti-vyāptm ity āh—śkti-smnvitm iti ॥60॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

एतच् च तत्त्वतो विष्णोः स्वरूपम् इति चिन्तनीयं, किन्तु तच्-छक्ति-व्याप्तम् इत्य् आह—शक्ति-समन्वितम् इति ॥६०॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 60).

... all of these which have the trividha bhaavanaas, brahma bhaavana, karma bhaavana, ubhaya bhaavana, this gross form of Paramaatman, though it can be meditated upon as it has a gross form, it has this bhaavanaa traya. Because of that, it is ashuddha, it is impure and not fit for meditation, it is not said to be Shubhaashraya, it is ashubhaashraya. Idam vishvam is the conclusion of whatever is told in this world. From Jagadetat charaacharam is a separate sentence. This is all said to be the shakti of Vishnu only. Shakti, amsha, tanu, vapu, shareera - all of these are paryaaya padaas, synonyms. This is controlled by Paramaatman, supported by Him, and meant for His purpose. He is the master of that. It is all shakti because it is supported by His sankalpa shakti. Just like our shareera, as our Jeevaatman is pervading the whole body, and is controlling by his willing only. In the same way, Paramaatman controls the whole world of sentients and non-sentients. There is another way of commenting on this, as told by some people. ## 61

संसारतापानखिलानवाप्नोत्यतिसंततान्

td — Everyone performs based; evm — on the karmaas.; āhyyeti — The kshetrajnya shakti,; vtutḥ — is covered by; srv-gtā — this third shakti, which; api — is avidyaa. Coming from beginningless time, without any break, they are continuously experiencing the grief of this samsaara, all the taapaas. ## 63

Śrīdhara

tārtmym evāh—aprāṇvtv ity-ādi ārdhaiś cturbhiḥ | aprāṇvtu anbhivykt-prāṇeṣu vlpā atyntm alpā, thāvreṣu alpā alpto'dhikā, arīṛpeṣu kṣudr-jntuṣu tto'py adhikā, ptttriṣu atiśktyā adhik-śktyā lkṣyte ॥64॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

तारतम्यम् एवाह—अप्राणवत्व् इत्य्-आदि आर्धैश् चतुर्भिः । अप्राणवतु अनभिव्यक्त-प्राणेषु वल्पा अत्यन्तम् अल्पा, थावरेषु अल्पा अल्पतोऽधिका, अरीऋपेषु क्षुद्र-जन्तुषु ततोऽप्य् अधिका, पतत्त्रिषु अतिशक्त्या अधिक-शक्त्या लक्ष्यते ॥६४॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 64).

The taaratamya, the relative differences are told here. In mountains, in wooden pieces, it is very very less. The jeevaatmans who are there in all these are of the same nature. and they are all very subtle, sookshma; and there is no vikaara in their svaroopa at any point of time, either in the bound state or any state. There is no vikaara in the essential nature of jeevaatman. It is only in the dharmabhoota jnyaana that the contraction and expansion happens, and differences exist among the jeevaatmans. Being less and more is not in the svaroopa, but in the jnyaana aananda dharma only; they experience less happiness, and their knowledge is limited. In serpents and other beings, it is a little more than stone, wood. In birds, it is still more. ## 65

VP 6.7.67

हिरण्यगर्भोऽपि ततः पुंसः शक्त्युपलक्षितः

Indra's dharmabhoota jnyaana is less contracted compared to the humans. Compared to all the devaas, the dharmabhoota jnyaana of Indra is much more. More or less means that it is covered by avidyaa. It is always in its original state, and in moksha also, it is not newly created. It is like a diamond or gem being covered by dust. When the dust is removed, it shines in its original state. The shine is not newly created. In the same way, the dharmabhoota jnyaana of all the sentient beings is covered with more or less dust, so that the dharmabhoota jnyaana is less or more expanded. The difference is only in this. Indra has the maximum dharmabhoota jnyaana compared to all the devaas. Prajaapati has more than Indra. Hiranyagarbha has much more. These are the relative differences in dharmabhoota jnyaana.

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

hirṇygrbho'pi — Indra's dharmabhoota jnyaana is less contracted compared to the humans. Compared to all the devaas, the dharmabhoota jnyaana of Indra is much more. More or less means that it is covered by avidyaa. It is; ttḥ — always in its original state, and in moksha also, it is not newly created. It is like a diamond or gem being covered by dust. When the dust is removed, it shines in its original; puṃsḥ — state. The shine is not newly created. In the same way, the dharmabhoota jnyaana of all the sentient beings is covered with more or less dust, so that the dharmabhoota jnyaana is less or more; śktyuplkṣitḥ — expanded. The difference is only in this. Indra has the maximum dharmabhoota jnyaana compared to all the devaas. Prajaapati has more than Indra. Hiranyagarbha has much more. These are the relative differences in dharmabhoota jnyaana.

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

hirṇygrbho'pi ttḥ puṃsḥ śktyuplkṣitḥ ||67||

Śrīdhara commentary.

No commentary.

VP 6.7.68

यतस्तच्छक्तियोगेन युक्तानि नभसा यथा

॥ Sri Engal Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

ytstcchktiyogen — ॥; yuktāni — Sri; nbhsā — Engal; ythā — Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Śrīdhara

asṅgtve'pi — Those who do not have praana in them, like mountain, which does not breathe, from apraani; srv-vyāptau — vastu to Hiranyagarbha, whatever were told, are all His shareera. Roopa, amsha, shakti, tanu, vapu, shareera are; dṛṣṭānto — all paryaaya padaas. Just as a man's body is pervaded by his sankalpa shakti, this entire thing; nbhsā — is pervaded by Bhagavaan's sankalpa shakti, and is controlled and supported by Him, by His mere sankalpa; ytheti — itself. By His mere willing, He can support and control everything. That is why it is said; 68 — to be His shareera. His sankalpa saamarthya is told here. His sankalpa is present everywhere. ## 69

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

ytstcchktiyogen yuktāni nbhsā ythā ||68||

Śrīdhara

asṅgtve'pi srv-vyāptau dṛṣṭānto nbhsā ytheti ॥68॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

असङ्गत्वेऽपि सर्व-व्याप्तौ दृष्टान्तो नभसा यथेति ॥६८॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 68).

Those who do not have praana in them, like mountain, which does not breathe, from apraani vastu to Hiranyagarbha, whatever were told, are all His shareera. Roopa, amsha, shakti, tanu, vapu, shareera are all paryaaya padaas. Just as a man's body is pervaded by his sankalpa shakti, this entire thing is pervaded by Bhagavaan's sankalpa shakti, and is controlled and supported by Him, by His mere sankalpa itself. By His mere willing, He can support and control everything. That is why it is said to be His shareera. His sankalpa saamarthya is told here. His sankalpa is present everywhere. ## 69

VP 6.7.72

चेष्टा तस्याप्रमेयस्य व्यापिन्यव्याहतात्मिका

॥ Sri Engal Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

ceṣṭā — ॥; tsyāprmeysy — Sri Engal; vyāpinyvyāhtātmikā — Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Śrīdhara

līlety — When He incarnates as a human,; uktṃ — He acts like a human. We can; smrthytejgtām — see this in Raama avataara, Krishna; iti — avataara. It is only for the good; krm-nimittāj — of the world that He does; jātā — that. It is not because He is; hi — experiencing the fruits of karma done; ceṣṭā — earlier. Because He is karmaadhyaksha, and not; pricchinnā — karma vashya. He is Sarveshvara, and; sprtighātā — not like a bound self. This is; c — there in all the jaatis -; sā — deva, manushya, etc. He is not born; tu — like the bound selves here, He; krm-nimittjā — incarnates out of His own will. Whatever; n — wonderful acts He did, leelaa cheshtitaas,; bhvti — in Brindaavana, Gokula, in controlling the Kaaliya; atḥ — sarpa, they are unopposed. Nobody can; vyāpinī — stop Him or oppose Him, or go; apricchinnā — against Him. Raavana, Shishupaala, Baana, and; avyāhtātmikā — others - nobody can stop or face; c — Him. He overpowers everyone. The avataaraas; atḥ — are said to be not karma nimitta,; sā — not for experiencing the fruits of; ceṣṭā — the karma, like the baddha jeevaas; the; svtntrsyāprmeysyeśvrsy — leelaas of the Lord do not; līlaivety — come under bhaavanaa traya, like the bound; arthḥ — selves, Because of this, the avataara; 72 — roopaas are also Shubhaashraya only. ## 73

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

ceṣṭā tsyāprmeysy vyāpinyvyāhtātmikā ||72||

Śrīdhara

līlety uktṃ smrthyte—jgtām iti | krm-nimittāj jātā hi ceṣṭā pricchinnā sprtighātā c sā tu krm-nimittjā n bhvti | atḥ vyāpinī apricchinnā avyāhtātmikā c | atḥ sā ceṣṭā svtntrsyāprmeysyeśvrsy līlaivety arthḥ ॥72॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

लीलेत्य् उक्तं समर्थयते—जगताम् इति । कर्म-निमित्ताज् जाता हि चेष्टा परिच्छिन्ना सप्रतिघाता च सा तु कर्म-निमित्तजा न भवति । अतः व्यापिनी अपरिच्छिन्ना अव्याहतात्मिका च । अतः सा चेष्टा स्वतन्त्रस्याप्रमेयस्येश्वरस्य लीलैवेत्य् अर्थः ॥७२॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 72).

When He incarnates as a human, He acts like a human. We can see this in Raama avataara, Krishna avataara. It is only for the good of the world that He does that. It is not because He is experiencing the fruits of karma done earlier. Because He is karmaadhyaksha, and not karma vashya. He is Sarveshvara, and not like a bound self. This is there in all the jaatis - deva, manushya, etc. He is not born like the bound selves here, He incarnates out of His own will. Whatever wonderful acts He did, leelaa cheshtitaas, in Brindaavana, Gokula, in controlling the Kaaliya sarpa, they are unopposed. Nobody can stop Him or oppose Him, or go against Him. Raavana, Shishupaala, Baana, and others - nobody can stop or face Him. He overpowers everyone. The avataaraas are said to be not karma nimitta, not for experiencing the fruits of the karma, like the baddha jeevaas; the leelaas of the Lord do not come under bhaavanaa traya, like the bound selves, Because of this, the avataara roopaas are also Shubhaashraya only. ## 73

Cleansing of the self means getting rid of the karmaas. One who is engaged in yoga has to meditate upon that. ## 74
