VP 6.7.76

त्रिभावभावनातीतो मुक्तये योगिनो नृप

of 194 Sri Vishnu Puraanam – Amsha 6 शुभाश्रयः

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

tribhāvbhāvnātīto — of 194; muktye — Sri Vishnu; yogino — Puraanam –; nṛp — Amsha 6 शुभाश्रयः

Śrīdhara

srvgtsyātmnḥ — The reason why the Shubhaashraya is beyond the tribhaavanaa, is told here.; pr-brhmṇo'py — It can be Shubhaashraya or Shuddhaashraya. Bhagavaan by Himself is sarvagata, He; āśryḥ — is all pervading, and He moves over everywhere. He is everywhere. He; prtiṣṭhā — is vyaapta, ananta. His svaroopa itself is vibhu. He also moves around; td — everywhere. With respect to something which is totally different, He is achala. His; uktṃ — vailakshanya is told here. He is different and distinct from everything else.; bhgvtā — That is why He is said to be Shubhaashraya. His avataara roopaas; gītāsu — are also beyond the bhaavanaa traya, and also they bestow liberation, moksha.; brhmṇo — They are moksha kaarana. Because of this, shubha and aashraya are both; hi — there. They can be meditated upon, and also they bestow the ultimate benefit,; prtiṣṭhāhm — moksha. This is told in Smruti and other places. Krishna says in; amṛtsyāvyysy — Bhagavad Geetha as "One who knows like this". One who establishes his; c — mind on Nrusimha, Vaamana - these avataara roopaas are also Shubhaashraya only.; śāśvtsy — In Saatvata Puraana, it is said that a man has ten indriyaas,; c — and is compared with Raavana, who has got ten heads, the manas itself; dhrmsy — is the raakshasa, like Raavana - the mind, with the ten sense; sukhsyaikāntiksy — organs, and with the bow and arrows which is nothing bur Viveka,; c — one can control the mind, and just as Sri Raama destroyed Raavana; gītā — with the power of His arrows, a yogi with viveka, upaasanaa, goes; 1427 — forth and controls all these sense organs. He is the one to be; iti — meditated upon, and His hue is like the sea. His eyes are; tribhāv-bhāvnātītḥ — blood red, and like a red lotus. He has got bow and; asṃsāritvāt — arrows. His Shubhaashraya is described, and His avataara roopaas are all to; 76 — be meditated upon by a yogi in order to get liberated. ## 77

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

tribhāvbhāvnātīto muktye yogino nṛp ||76||

Śrīdhara

srvgtsyātmnḥ pr-brhmṇo'py āśryḥ prtiṣṭhā | td uktṃ bhgvtā gītāsu— brhmṇo hi prtiṣṭhāhm amṛtsyāvyysy c | śāśvtsy c dhrmsy sukhsyaikāntiksy c ॥ [gītā 14.27] iti | tribhāv-bhāvnātītḥ asṃsāritvāt ॥76॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

सर्वगतस्यात्मनः पर-ब्रह्मणोऽप्य् आश्रयः प्रतिष्ठा । तद् उक्तं भगवता गीतासु— ब्रह्मणो हि प्रतिष्ठाहम् अमृतस्याव्ययस्य च । शाश्वतस्य च धर्मस्य सुखस्यैकान्तिकस्य च ॥ [गीता १४.२७] इति । त्रिभाव-भावनातीतः असंसारित्वात् ॥७६॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 76).

The reason why the Shubhaashraya is beyond the tribhaavanaa, is told here. It can be Shubhaashraya or Shuddhaashraya. Bhagavaan by Himself is sarvagata, He is all pervading, and He moves over everywhere. He is everywhere. He is vyaapta, ananta. His svaroopa itself is vibhu. He also moves around everywhere. With respect to something which is totally different, He is achala. His vailakshanya is told here. He is different and distinct from everything else. That is why He is said to be Shubhaashraya. His avataara roopaas are also beyond the bhaavanaa traya, and also they bestow liberation, moksha. They are moksha kaarana. Because of this, shubha and aashraya are both there. They can be meditated upon, and also they bestow the ultimate benefit, moksha. This is told in Smruti and other places. Krishna says in Bhagavad Geetha as "One who knows like this". One who establishes his mind on Nrusimha, Vaamana - these avataara roopaas are also Shubhaashraya only. In Saatvata Puraana, it is said that a man has ten indriyaas, and is compared with Raavana, who has got ten heads, the manas itself is the raakshasa, like Raavana - the mind, with the ten sense organs, and with the bow and arrows which is nothing bur Viveka, one can control the mind, and just as Sri Raama destroyed Raavana with the power of His arrows, a yogi with viveka, upaasanaa, goes forth and controls all these sense organs. He is the one to be meditated upon, and His hue is like the sea. His eyes are blood red, and like a red lotus. He has got bow and arrows. His Shubhaashraya is described, and His avataara roopaas are all to be meditated upon by a yogi in order to get liberated. ## 77

Śrīdhara

anydevtā-nindyā — All others are ashuddhaas, it is said. The baddha and mukta roopaas told, the moorta and amoorta roopaas, are all ashuddhaas, are ashubhaashrayaas, they are different from bhagavat; bhgvn-mūrti-dhārṇām — roopa. It is told clearly that from Chaturmukha Brahma to the lowest being, which are all there in this prakruti mandala, which are having a body, and are baddha,; ev — are all born because of the fruits of experiencing their karma. They are all under the sway of samsaara, going in the cycle of birth and death. All the; dṛḍhīkrotianye — baddha jeevaas are not fit for dhyaana by a yogi. They themselves are having avidyaa, karma, and they are in samsaara, so they cannot be meditated upon by; tv — one who is desirous of liberation, moksha. Those who have escaped this samsaara, and have got rid of all their karma, their jnyaana is in its proper full state,; ity — original state, they have got the jnyaana; they are also not useful for one who is engaged in dhyaana; because their jnyaana is not natural; they were bound selves; ādi — at one time, and with Bhagavaan's anugraha, His grace, they got rid of this karma, and then they became muktaas; so the jnyaana was always not there for; dvābhyām — them. So, the Brahman who is eternally having asankuchita jnyaana, Satyam jnyaanam anantam Brahma, is only to be meditated upon, as told by Shounaka. This is also told in; 77 — the Mahaabhaarata, that they are all in this samsaara only. Manu, Mahaabhaarata, and Puraanaas are given as pramaana here. Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

aśuddhāste smstāstu devādyāḥ krmyonyḥ ||77||

Śrīdhara

anydevtā-nindyā bhgvn-mūrti-dhārṇām ev dṛḍhīkroti—anye tv ity ādi dvābhyām ॥77॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

अन्यदेवता-निन्दया भगवन्-मूर्ति-धारणाम् एव दृढीकरोति—अन्ये त्व् इत्य् आदि द्वाभ्याम् ॥७७॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 77).

All others are ashuddhaas, it is said. The baddha and mukta roopaas told, the moorta and amoorta roopaas, are all ashuddhaas, are ashubhaashrayaas, they are different from bhagavat roopa. It is told clearly that from Chaturmukha Brahma to the lowest being, which are all there in this prakruti mandala, which are having a body, and are baddha, are all born because of the fruits of experiencing their karma. They are all under the sway of samsaara, going in the cycle of birth and death. All the baddha jeevaas are not fit for dhyaana by a yogi. They themselves are having avidyaa, karma, and they are in samsaara, so they cannot be meditated upon by one who is desirous of liberation, moksha. Those who have escaped this samsaara, and have got rid of all their karma, their jnyaana is in its proper full state, original state, they have got the jnyaana; they are also not useful for one who is engaged in dhyaana; because their jnyaana is not natural; they were bound selves at one time, and with Bhagavaan's anugraha, His grace, they got rid of this karma, and then they became muktaas; so the jnyaana was always not there for them. So, the Brahman who is eternally having asankuchita jnyaana, Satyam jnyaanam anantam Brahma, is only to be meditated upon, as told by Shounaka. This is also told in the Mahaabhaarata, that they are all in this samsaara only. Manu, Mahaabhaarata, and Puraanaas are given as pramaana here. They are told as ashuddhaas because of karmayonitva. ## 78

VP 6.7.79

तच्छ्रूयतामनाधारा धारमा नोपपद्यते

O King, what is the sthoola roopa of Bhagavaan? How is it to be meditated upon? That I am going to tell you, listen to me. Keshidhvaja said. Dhaaranaa cannot be done without thinking about a form in the mind. Something has to be established in the mind. The mind needs an aadhaara for dhaaranaa. One has to establish a form in the mind, and then meditate upon it. Without that, it is not possible. What is the form to be established in the mind for dhyaana, is now told as the Bhagavad roopa, the divya mangala vigraha, the most wonderful description of Bhagavat divya mangala vigraha.

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

tcchrūytāmnādhārā — O King, what is the sthoola roopa of Bhagavaan? How is it to be meditated upon? That I am going to tell you, listen to me. Keshidhvaja said. Dhaaranaa cannot be done without thinking about; dhārmā — a form in the mind. Something has to be established in the mind. The mind needs an aadhaara for dhaaranaa. One has to establish a form in the mind, and then meditate upon it. Without that,; noppdyte — it is not possible. What is the form to be established in the mind for dhyaana, is now told as the Bhagavad roopa, the divya mangala vigraha, the most wonderful description of Bhagavat divya mangala vigraha.

VP 6.7.86

तावद्यावद्दृढीभूता तत्रैव नृप धारणा

With complete concentration, the yogi has to meditate on the form of the Lord with the Shaarnga, Shankha, the Varada Abhaya hasta, etc., keeping his mind tranquil and equipoised. He has to meditate upon this as long as he has firmly established the form, he has to think of only this and nothing else. This is dhaaranaa.

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

tāvdyāvddṛḍhībhūtā — With complete concentration, the yogi has to meditate on the form of the Lord; ttraiv — with the Shaarnga, Shankha, the Varada Abhaya hasta, etc., keeping his mind tranquil and; nṛp — equipoised. He has to meditate upon this as long as he has firmly established; dhārṇā — the form, he has to think of only this and nothing else. This is dhaaranaa.

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

tāvdyāvddṛḍhībhūtā ttraiv nṛp dhārṇā ||86||

Śrīdhara commentary.

No commentary.

VP 6.7.87

नापयाति यदा चित्तत्सिद्धां मन्येत तां तदा

Whether is walking or standing, or whatever he is doing, whatever work he is engaged in, when such a form of Bhagavaan, the wonderful divya mangala vigraha of Bhagavaan does not go away from his mind, is always established in his mind, whatever he may be doing, walking, sitting, standing, or doing any other work, that is when he can understand that his dhaaranaa is now mastered, that he has attained the state of dhaaranaa.

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

nāpyāti — Whether is walking or standing, or whatever he is doing, whatever work; ydā — he is engaged in, when such a form of Bhagavaan, the wonderful divya; citttsiddhāṃ — mangala vigraha of Bhagavaan does not go away from his mind, is; mnyet — always established in his mind, whatever he may be doing, walking, sitting, standing,; tāṃ — or doing any other work, that is when he can understand that; tdā — his dhaaranaa is now mastered, that he has attained the state of dhaaranaa.

Śrīdhara

dhārṇā-dārḍhy-lkṣṇm — Whether is walking or standing,; āhvrjt — or whatever he is doing, whatever; iti — work he is engaged in,; cittād — when such a form of Bhagavaan,; ydā — the wonderful divya mangala vigraha; mūrtir — of Bhagavaan does not go away; nāpyāti — from his mind, is always; tdā — established in his mind, whatever he; tāṃ — may be doing, walking, sitting,; dhārānāṃ — standing, or doing any other work,; siddhāṃ — that is when he can; dṛḍhāṃ — understand that his dhaaranaa is now; mnyet — mastered, that he has attained; 87 — the state of dhaaranaa. ## 88

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

nāpyāti ydā citttsiddhāṃ mnyet tāṃ tdā ||87||

Śrīdhara

dhārṇā-dārḍhy-lkṣṇm āh—vrjt iti | cittād ydā mūrtir nāpyāti, tdā tāṃ dhārānāṃ siddhāṃ dṛḍhāṃ mnyet ॥87॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

धारणा-दार्ढ्य-लक्षणम् आह—व्रजत इति । चित्ताद् यदा मूर्तिर् नापयाति, तदा तां धारानां सिद्धां दृढां मन्येत ॥८७॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 87).

Whether is walking or standing, or whatever he is doing, whatever work he is engaged in, when such a form of Bhagavaan, the wonderful divya mangala vigraha of Bhagavaan does not go away from his mind, is always established in his mind, whatever he may be doing, walking, sitting, standing, or doing any other work, that is when he can understand that his dhaaranaa is now mastered, that he has attained the state of dhaaranaa. ## 88

The Bhagavaan's form is to be meditated upon, with sphatika akshamaalaa, yajnyopaveeta, etc. ## 89

VP 6.7.90

कुर्यात्ततोऽवयविनि प्रणिधानपरो भवेत्

॥ Sri Engal Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

kuryāttto'vyvini — ॥; prṇidhānpro — Sri Engal; bhvet — Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Śrīdhara

ekāvyvṃ — Then,; pād-gulphādi-nānāvyv-niṣṭhṃ — he has; citt-dārḍhym — to think; ektr — of each; niyojy — avayava of; ekāvyvṃ — Bhagavaan. This; cetsā — is the; kuryāt — fourth dhaaranaa,; td — with the; uktṃ — angaas; bhāgvte — of Bhagavaan.; tt — The aayudhaas,; srv-vyāpkṃ — bhooshanaas, the; cittm — various angaas; ākṛṣyaiktr — of Bhagavaan,; dhāryet — with the; nānyāni — continuous practice; cintyed — and meditation,; bhūyḥ — when; susmitṃ — it is; bhāvyen — firmly established; mukhm — in the; bhāpu — mind, and; 111443 — then only; iti — one avayava,; td — having the; evṃ — Bhagavaan's form,; līlā-dhārṇaiv — this; sthūl-sūkṣm-krmeṇ — is to; cturdhoktā — be meditated; idānīṃ — upon. This; tt-kṛtt — is giving; evśrīr-dhārṇām — importance to; āh — one avayava; prsnn-cāru-vdnm — at a; ity — time, and; ādinoktāvyvā — then; yen — this is; līlā-mūrtinā — told as; āviṣkṛtās — dhyaana. Till; tsmin — this, it; avyvini — is dhaaranaa,; yd — and now; vā — it is; avyvā — dhyaana. The; līlāvtārā — avayavi; yen — is said; gṛhītāḥ — to be; tsmin — of gomukhaakaara,; prṇidhānṃ — and one; citt-sthairyṃ — has to; tt-pro — do dhyaana; bhvet — in that.; 90 — ## 91

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

kuryāttto'vyvini prṇidhānpro bhvet ||90||

Śrīdhara

ekāvyvṃ pād-gulphādi-nānāvyv-niṣṭhṃ citt-dārḍhym | ektr niyojy ekāvyvṃ cetsā kuryāt | td uktṃ bhāgvte— tt srv-vyāpkṃ cittm ākṛṣyaiktr dhāryet | nānyāni cintyed bhūyḥ susmitṃ bhāvyen mukhm [bhā.pu. 11.14.43] iti | td evṃ līlā-dhārṇaiv sthūl-sūkṣm-krmeṇ cturdhoktā | idānīṃ tt-kṛtt evśrīr-dhārṇām āh | prsnn-cāru-vdnm ity ādinoktāvyvā yen līlā-mūrtinā āviṣkṛtās tsmin avyvini | yd vā, avyvā līlāvtārā yen gṛhītāḥ, tsmin prṇidhānṃ citt-sthairyṃ tt-pro bhvet ॥90॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

एकावयवं पाद-गुल्फादि-नानावयव-निष्ठं चित्त-दार्ढ्यम् । एकत्र नियोज्य एकावयवं चेतसा कुर्यात् । तद् उक्तं भागवते— तत् सर्व-व्यापकं चित्तम् आकृष्यैकत्र धारयेत् । नान्यानि चिन्तयेद् भूयः सुस्मितं भावयेन् मुखम् [भा.पु. ११.१४.४३] इति। तद् एवं लीला-धारणैव स्थूल-सूक्ष्म-क्रमेण चतुर्धोक्ता । इदानीं तत्-कृतत एवशरीर-धारणाम् आह । प्रसन्न-चारु-वदनम् इत्य् आदिनोक्तावयवा येन लीला-मूर्तिना आविष्कृतास् तस्मिन् अवयविनि । यद् वा, अवयवा लीलावतारा येन गृहीताः, तस्मिन् प्रणिधानं चित्त-स्थैर्यं तत्-परो भवेत् ॥९०॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 90).

Then, he has to think of each avayava of Bhagavaan. This is the fourth dhaaranaa, with the angaas of Bhagavaan. The aayudhaas, bhooshanaas, the various angaas of Bhagavaan, with the continuous practice and meditation, when it is firmly established in the mind, and then only one avayava, having the Bhagavaan's form, this is to be meditated upon. This is giving importance to one avayava at a time, and then this is told as dhyaana. Till this, it is dhaaranaa, and now it is dhyaana. The avayavi is said to be of gomukhaakaara, and one has to do dhyaana in that. ## 91

VP 6.7.91

तद्ध्यानं प्रथमैरङ्गैः षड्भिर्निष्पाद्यते नृप

॥ Sri Engal Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

tddhyānṃ — ॥; prthmairṅgaiḥ — Sri; ṣḍbhirniṣpādyte — Engal; nṛp — Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Śrīdhara

idānīṃ — This is; dhyānm — the form of; āhtd-rūpeti — dhyaana. When once; td-rūpsyaiv — dhaaranaa is established,; dhārṇā — this is the; siddhsyaiv — same as tailadhaaraavat; vstunḥ — avicchinna smruti santati; prtyyo — roopaa, what Bhaashyakaarar; ysyāṃ — says, that there; snttau — should be no; sā — other thought coming; ekāvicchinnā — in between two; snttiḥ — thoughts. This is; any-niḥspṛhā — saalambana yoga. In; viṣyāntreṇ — the astra bhooshana; avyvdhīymānā — adhyaaya, the form; vijātīy-prtyyānntrit-sjātīy-prtyy-prvāho — that is meditated; dhyānm — upon, with astra,; ity — bhooshana, is said; arthḥ — to be sabeeja; yc — yoga. The dhyaana; c — is now established; prthmair — with the six; ymādibhir — angaas - yama,; dhārṇāntaiḥ — niyama, aasana, praanaayaama,; ṣḍbhir — pratyaahaara, dhaaranaa. Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

tddhyānṃ prthmairṅgaiḥ ṣḍbhirniṣpādyte nṛp ||91||

Śrīdhara

idānīṃ dhyānm āh—td-rūpeti | td-rūpsyaiv dhārṇā siddhsyaiv vstunḥ prtyyo ysyāṃ snttau sā ekāvicchinnā snttiḥ | any-niḥspṛhā viṣyāntreṇ avyvdhīymānā vijātīy-prtyyānntrit-sjātīy-prtyy-prvāho dhyānm ity arthḥ | yc c prthmair ymādibhir dhārṇāntaiḥ ṣḍbhir aṅgair niṣpādyte ॥91॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

इदानीं ध्यानम् आह—तद्-रूपेति । तद्-रूपस्यैव धारणा सिद्धस्यैव वस्तुनः प्रत्ययो यस्यां सन्ततौ सा एकाविच्छिन्ना सन्ततिः । अन्य-निःस्पृहा विषयान्तरेण अव्यवधीयमाना विजातीय-प्रत्ययानन्तरित-सजातीय-प्रत्यय-प्रवाहो ध्यानम् इत्य् अर्थः । यच् च प्रथमैर् यमादिभिर् धारणान्तैः षड्भिर् अङ्गैर् निष्पाद्यते ॥९१॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 91).

This is the form of dhyaana. When once dhaaranaa is established, this is the same as tailadhaaraavat avicchinna smruti santati roopaa, what Bhaashyakaarar says, that there should be no other thought coming in between two thoughts. This is saalambana yoga. In the astra bhooshana adhyaaya, the form that is meditated upon, with astra, bhooshana, is said to be sabeeja yoga. The dhyaana is now established with the six angaas - yama, niyama, aasana, praanaayaama, pratyaahaara, dhaaranaa. Now is told the dhyaana. Samaadhi is next. ## 92

idṃ — It is not only that it; jñānṃ — will lead one to Brahman, but also; n — will make one get liberated. He has to do until; āhkṣetrjñ — he gets moksha, aharahah anustheya, this bhakti; iti — yoga he has to perform everyday,; kṣetrjñḥ — he will attain moksha. Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

niṣpādy muktikāryṃ vai kṛtkṛtyṃ nivrtte ||94||

Śrīdhara

idṃ jñānṃ n kevlṃ brhm-prāpk-mātrṃ, kiṃ tu mukti-kārṇm apīty āh—kṣetrjñ iti | kṣetrjñḥ anādi-kāl-kṣetr-rūp-māyā-guṇ-try-tirohit-sv-svrūpḥ krṇī krṇvān smādhi-rūpṃ jñānṃ krṇṃ tj-jñānṃ ten krṇtven tsy kṣetrjñsy mukti-kāryṃ niṣpādy kṛt-kṛtyṃ nivrtte, muktim akṛtvā n nivrtt ity arthḥ ॥94॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

इदं ज्ञानं न केवलं ब्रह्म-प्रापक-मात्रं, किं तु मुक्ति-कारणम् अपीत्य् आह—क्षेत्रज्ञ इति । क्षेत्रज्ञः अनादि-काल-क्षेत्र-रूप-माया-गुण-त्रय-तिरोहित-स्व-स्वरूपः करणी करणवान् समाधि-रूपं ज्ञानं करणं तज्-ज्ञानं तेन करणत्वेन तस्य क्षेत्रज्ञस्य मुक्ति-कार्यं निष्पाद्य कृत-कृत्यं निवर्तते, मुक्तिम् अकृत्वा न निवर्तत इत्य् अर्थः ॥९४॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 94).

It is not only that it will lead one to Brahman, but also will make one get liberated. Kshetrajnya is the jeevaatman, upaasaka, who is meditating, is the karani. The karana, saadhana is jnyaana, the upaasana. It will definitely give him the fruit of mukti, and then only will it return, it is told here. He has to do until he gets moksha, aharahah anustheya, this bhakti yoga he has to perform everyday, he will attain moksha. The prakruti, which is guna traya, called maayaa, in the form of kshetra, the shareera, his svaroopa is covered by that. Karani is one who performs the upaasanaa. Karana, the instrument is upaasanaa, the samaadhi roopa jnyaana. It will not end before getting him liberation. He has to perform this till he gets liberation. It will get him liberation, and he will never return to samsaara again. Kshetrajnya is one whose real nature is covered by the veil of prakruti, and maayaa which is trigunaatmaka, consisting of sattva, rajas, tamas, since beginningless time. Karani means one who performs, executes the means to moksha. The jnyaana of the form of samaadhi, is the instrument, the means. It will not get over before he gets liberation. It will take him up to that. This is the power of samaadhi, he says. Now, the moksha svaroopa is told in the next shloka. ## 95

VP 6.7.96

आत्मनो ब्रह्मणो भेदमसंतं कः करीष्यति

॥ Sri Engal Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

ātmno — ॥; brhmṇo — Sri; bhedmsṃtṃ — Engal; kḥ — Aalwaan’s; krīṣyti — Commentary:

Śrīdhara

ajñāne — The moksha svaroopa is only explained here. The differences with respect to deva, manushya, etc., his karma gets completely; vinṣṭe — destroyed when he sees Paramaatman and goes and joins Him. The ajnyaana, ignorance of the nature of karma, is the; sti — cause of various differences like various forms of deva, manushya, etc. When that is destroyed completely with the darshana of; n — Paramaatman, with the aatman being jnyaanaika roopa itself, then who will say that he is deva, or he is manushya,; punḥ — then the aatma Paramaatma saamya with respect to jnyaana aakaaratva svaroopa and in the jnyaana dharma, the similarity will be; sṃsār-prroh-śṅkety — there. Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

ātmno brhmṇo bhedmsṃtṃ kḥ krīṣyti ||96||

Śrīdhara

ajñāne vinṣṭe sti n punḥ sṃsār-prroh-śṅkety āh—vibhedeti ॥96॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

अज्ञाने विनष्टे सति न पुनः संसार-प्ररोह-शङ्केत्य् आह—विभेदेति ॥९६॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 96).

The moksha svaroopa is only explained here. The differences with respect to deva, manushya, etc., his karma gets completely destroyed when he sees Paramaatman and goes and joins Him. The ajnyaana, ignorance of the nature of karma, is the cause of various differences like various forms of deva, manushya, etc. When that is destroyed completely with the darshana of Paramaatman, with the aatman being jnyaanaika roopa itself, then who will say that he is deva, or he is manushya, then the aatma Paramaatma saamya with respect to jnyaana aakaaratva svaroopa and in the jnyaana dharma, the similarity will be there. That jeevaatman is anu, and Paramaatman is vibhu will remain, but both will be of the nature of consciousness. One will not differentiate like deva, manushya. Shounaka's words are also told as pramaana here. The four kinds of bheda told here - deva, manushya, tiryak, sthaavara, is because of the ajnyaana which is happening due to karma. ## 97

I have told you briefly and also in detail. What else do you want to know?"

of 194 Sri Vishnu Puraanam – Amsha 6 Khaandikya replied - O Keshidhvaja, with your teaching of this yoga, everything is done. I am happy, there is nothing else I want. VP 6.7.99

नरेन्द्र गदितुं शक्यमपि विज्ञेयवेदिभिः

॥ Sri Engal Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Śrīdhara

kuto — I said "My, mine,; mm — that I have heard; tvopdeśenety — everything, my mind has now; etd — been cleared". These words; vcnṃ — "I, mine", without using; vidyt — these words, I cannot convey; ity — you these. That is; at — why I told, and; āhmmeti — not with any other reason.; mmeti — I have lost this; yn — ahamkaara, mamakaara. If I; myā — tell that my mind is; proktm — pure, then it may; etd — mean that I still; asd — have ahamkaara, but that is; ev — not what I am; bādhitānuvṛttyā — meaning here. I am; proktm — not having any ahamkaara, mamakaara.; anythā — When the impurities are; tu — all destroyed, I still; vijñey-vedibhiḥ — have to tell that "My; avgtprmārthair — mind". Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

nrendr gdituṃ śkympi vijñeyvedibhiḥ ||99||

Śrīdhara

kuto mm tvopdeśenety etd vcnṃ vidyt ity at āh—mmeti | mmeti yn myā proktm etd asd ev bādhitānuvṛttyā proktm | anythā tu vijñey-vedibhiḥ avgtprmārthair gditum api n śkyṃ, kutḥ punḥ prārbdh-krm-bhogs teṣām ity arthḥ ॥99॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

कुतो मम तवोपदेशेनेत्य् एतद् वचनं विद्यत इत्य् अत आह—ममेति । ममेति यन् मया प्रोक्तम् एतद् असद् एव बाधितानुवृत्त्या प्रोक्तम् । अन्यथा तु विज्ञेय-वेदिभिः अवगतपरमार्थैर् गदितुम् अपि न शक्यं, कुतः पुनः प्रारब्ध-कर्म-भोगस् तेषाम् इत्य् अर्थः ॥९९॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 99).

I said "My, mine, that I have heard everything, my mind has now been cleared". These words "I, mine", without using these words, I cannot convey you these. That is why I told, and not with any other reason. I have lost this ahamkaara, mamakaara. If I tell that my mind is pure, then it may mean that I still have ahamkaara, but that is not what I am meaning here. I am not having any ahamkaara, mamakaara. When the impurities are all destroyed, I still have to tell that "My mind". When the complete impurities are destroyed, I should not even be using aham, mama, but those who have understood the truth, and are trying to tell, even those who have realized the ultimate, cannot convey this without using those words. That is why I am using that, he says. ## 100

VP 6.7.100

परमार्थस्त्वसंलाप्यो गोचरे वचसां न यः

॥ Sri Engal Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

prmārthstvsṃlāpyo — ॥; gocre — Sri; vcsāṃ — Engal; n — Aalwaan’s; yḥ — Commentary:

Śrīdhara

tt — Aham, mama are avidyaa jnyaana, thinking one as something else. He is a man, she is a woman, this is a cow, horse, is also avidyaa, it is not the right; spṣṭytiahṃ — knowledge, because all are jnyaana aakaaraas, aatmans only. All the differences, what I said, are due to avidyaa only, bhramaa. It is the wrong knowledge. The truth is that aatman is eternal,; mmeti — and we cannot express by words. We cannot talk about him. We cannot say that this aatman is goat, this is buffalo, etc; we cannot use these words to indicate an; 100 — aatman. Because all aatmans are jnyaana aakaaraas only. He cannot be told using these words. Because these are all coming due to prakruti sambandha and not in his real nature. ## 101

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

prmārthstvsṃlāpyo gocre vcsāṃ n yḥ ||100||

Śrīdhara

tt spṣṭyti—ahṃ mmeti ॥100॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

तत् स्पष्टयति—अहं ममेति ॥१००॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 100).

Aham, mama are avidyaa jnyaana, thinking one as something else. He is a man, she is a woman, this is a cow, horse, is also avidyaa, it is not the right knowledge, because all are jnyaana aakaaraas, aatmans only. All the differences, what I said, are due to avidyaa only, bhramaa. It is the wrong knowledge. The truth is that aatman is eternal, and we cannot express by words. We cannot talk about him. We cannot say that this aatman is goat, this is buffalo, etc; we cannot use these words to indicate an aatman. Because all aatmans are jnyaana aakaaraas only. He cannot be told using these words. Because these are all coming due to prakruti sambandha and not in his real nature. ## 101

VP 6.7.101

यद्विमुक्तिप्रदो योगः प्रोक्तः केशिध्वजाव्ययः

You can return, O Keshidhvaja. You told me everything for my good, shreyas. Shreyas is told in Upanishads as the reality principle, and not preyas, the pleasure principle. You have taught me all these for my ultimate good. You have taught me the yoga which will get me ultimate liberation itself. You have taught me the eternal moksha itself - the means to that, O Keshidhvaja.

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

You have taught me all; proktḥ — these for my ultimate good. You have taught me the yoga which will get me ultimate; keśidhvjāvyyḥ — liberation itself. You have taught me the eternal moksha itself - the means to that, O Keshidhvaja.

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

VP 6.7.103

वनं जगाम गोविन्दे विनिवेशितमानसः

Khaandikya also made his son as the king, and in order to perfect and practice his yoga, which was taught by Keshidhvaja, he went to the forest, having fixed his mind in Govinda. He wanted to meditate, do upaasanaa, and attain moksha. So, he retired to the forest.

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

vnṃ — Khaandikya also made his son as the king, and in order to; jgām — perfect and practice his yoga, which was taught by Keshidhvaja, he went; govinde — to the forest, having fixed his mind in Govinda. He wanted to; viniveśitmānsḥ — meditate, do upaasanaa, and attain moksha. So, he retired to the forest.

Śrīdhara

rājānṃ — Khaandikya also made his son as the king, and in; vn-durg-mntri-bhṛtyādi-svāminṃ — order to perfect and practice his yoga, which was taught; sutṃ — by Keshidhvaja, he went to the forest, having fixed his; kṛtvā — mind in Govinda. He wanted to meditate, do upaasanaa, and; 103 — attain moksha. So, he retired to the forest. ## 104

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

vnṃ jgām govinde viniveśitmānsḥ ||103||

Śrīdhara

rājānṃ vn-durg-mntri-bhṛtyādi-svāminṃ sutṃ kṛtvā ॥103॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

राजानं वन-दुर्ग-मन्त्रि-भृत्यादि-स्वामिनं सुतं कृत्वा ॥१०३॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 103).

Khaandikya also made his son as the king, and in order to perfect and practice his yoga, which was taught by Keshidhvaja, he went to the forest, having fixed his mind in Govinda. He wanted to meditate, do upaasanaa, and attain moksha. So, he retired to the forest. ## 104

VP 6.7.105

बुभुजे विषयान्कर्म चक्रे चानभिसंहितम्

of 194 Sri Vishnu Puraanam – Amsha 6

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

bubhuje — of 194; viṣyānkrm — Sri Vishnu; ckre — Puraanam –; cānbhisṃhitm — Amsha 6

Śrīdhara

mukty-arthṃ — Keshidhvaja, for the purpose of liberation, in order to destroy all his karmaas which were; videh-kaivlyārthm — there, he performed acts without any desire for fruits therein, and with the mamataa tyaaga,; anbhisṃhitṃ — kartrutva tyaaga, sambandha tyaaga, phala tyaaga, without any desire in the fruits, and with Bhagavat aaraadhana; phlābhisndhi-śūnyṃ — roopa. And he also enjoyed based on the karma, without any attachment, because it has; lok-sṅgrhārthṃ — to be destroyed. The praarabdha karmaas, which have started to give fruits, have to be; krm — experienced. But there should be no attachment to any of that. If something bad happens, he; ckre — will experience it, but he will not have any attachment to the fruits. If something; 105 — good happens also, he will experience without any attachment. Keshidhvaja was doing like this. ## 106

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

bubhuje viṣyānkrm ckre cānbhisṃhitm ||105||

Śrīdhara

mukty-arthṃ videh-kaivlyārthm anbhisṃhitṃ phlābhisndhi-śūnyṃ lok-sṅgrhārthṃ krm ckre ॥105॥

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

मुक्त्य्-अर्थं विदेह-कैवल्यार्थम् अनभिसंहितं फलाभिसन्धि-शून्यं लोक-सङ्ग्रहार्थं कर्म चक्रे ॥१०५॥

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 105).

Keshidhvaja, for the purpose of liberation, in order to destroy all his karmaas which were there, he performed acts without any desire for fruits therein, and with the mamataa tyaaga, kartrutva tyaaga, sambandha tyaaga, phala tyaaga, without any desire in the fruits, and with Bhagavat aaraadhana roopa. And he also enjoyed based on the karma, without any attachment, because it has to be destroyed. The praarabdha karmaas, which have started to give fruits, have to be experienced. But there should be no attachment to any of that. If something bad happens, he will experience it, but he will not have any attachment to the fruits. If something good happens also, he will experience without any attachment. Keshidhvaja was doing like this. ## 106

VP 6.7.106

अवाप सिद्धिमत्यन्तां तापक्षयफलां द्विज

॥ Sri Engal Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Word-for-word
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

avāp — ॥; siddhimtyntāṃ — Sri; tāpkṣyphlāṃ — Engal; dvij — Aalwaan’s Commentary:

Śrīdhara

kṣīṇ-pāpḥ — Having got destroyed all the praarabdha karmaas,; kṣīṇ-prārbdh-krmā — which had started to give fruit already, and; 106 — which cannot be avoided, having experienced them, and; iti — having got rid of all the paapaas having; viṣṇu-purāṇe — experienced them, which had already started to give; ṣṣṭhe'ṃśe — fruits, having become amala, having got his praarabdha; sptmo'dhyāyḥ — karma destroyed fully, then he became eligible for; o0o — atyanta siddhi, liberation. This completes Chapter Seven. ॥

Transliteration
Viṣṇu Purāṇa

avāp siddhimtyntāṃ tāpkṣyphlāṃ dvij ||106||

Śrīdhara

kṣīṇ-pāpḥ kṣīṇ-prārbdh-krmā ॥106॥ ॥ iti viṣṇu-purāṇe ṣṣṭhe'ṃśe sptmo'dhyāyḥ ॥ —o)0(o—

Śrīdhara Sanskrit

क्षीण-पापः क्षीण-प्रारब्ध-कर्मा ॥१०६॥ ॥ इति विष्णु-पुराणे षष्ठेऽंशे सप्तमोऽध्यायः ॥ —ओ)०(ओ—

Śrīdhara commentary (source range 106).

Having got destroyed all the praarabdha karmaas, which had started to give fruit already, and which cannot be avoided, having experienced them, and having got rid of all the paapaas having experienced them, which had already started to give fruits, having become amala, having got his praarabdha karma destroyed fully, then he became eligible for atyanta siddhi, liberation. This completes Chapter Seven. ॥
